### Generate PIT Table using YAML Metadata

Source: https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/metadata

This snippet demonstrates how to generate a Point-in-Time (PIT) table using the `automate_dv.pit` macro. It takes a YAML string defining source model, primary key, as-of-date table, satellites, and stage table load dates. The macro processes this metadata to create the PIT table structure.

```jinja
{% set yaml_metadata -%}
source_model: hub_customer
src_pk: CUSTOMER_HK
as_of_dates_table: AS_OF_DATE
satellites: 
  SAT_CUSTOMER_DETAILS:
    pk:
      PK: CUSTOMER_HK
    ldts:
      LDTS: LOAD_DATETIME
  SAT_CUSTOMER_LOGIN:
    pk:
      PK: CUSTOMER_HK
    ldts:
      LDTS: LOAD_DATETIME
  SAT_CUSTOMER_PROFILE:
    pk:
      PK: CUSTOMER_HK
    ldts:
      LDTS: LOAD_DATETIME
stage_tables_ldts:
  STG_CUSTOMER_DETAILS: LOAD_DATETIME
  STG_CUSTOMER_LOGIN: LOAD_DATETIME
  STG_CUSTOMER_PROFILE: LOAD_DATETIME
src_ldts: LOAD_DATETIME
{% endset %}

{% set metadata_dict = fromyaml(yaml_metadata) %}

{{ automate_dv.pit(source_model=metadata_dict['source_model'], 
                   src_pk=metadata_dict['src_pk'],
                   as_of_dates_table=metadata_dict['as_of_dates_table'],
                   satellites=metadata_dict['satellites'],
                   stage_tables_ldts=metadata_dict['stage_tables_ldts'],
                   src_ldts=metadata_dict['src_ldts']) }}

```

--------------------------------

### Generate PIT Customer Table with dbt

Source: https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/tutorial/tut_point_in_time

This dbt SQL snippet demonstrates how to generate an incremental PIT table using the 'automate_dv' package. It parses YAML metadata and calls the 'pit' macro with the relevant configurations.

```sql
{{ config(materialized='pit_incremental') }}

{%- set yaml_metadata -%}
source_model: hub_customer
src_pk: CUSTOMER_HK
as_of_dates_table: AS_OF_DATE
satellites: 
  SAT_CUSTOMER_DETAILS:
    pk:
      PK: CUSTOMER_HK
    ldts:
      LDTS: LOAD_DATETIME
  SAT_CUSTOMER_LOGIN:
    pk:
      PK: CUSTOMER_HK
    ldts:
      LDTS: LOAD_DATETIME
stage_tables: 
  STG_CUSTOMER_DETAILS: LOAD_DATETIME
  STG_CUSTOMER_LOGIN: LOAD_DATETIME    
src_ldts: LOAD_DATETIME
{%- endset -%}

{% set metadata_dict = fromyaml(yaml_metadata) %}

{% set source_model = metadata_dict['source_model'] %}
{% set src_pk = metadata_dict['src_pk'] %}
{% set as_of_dates_table = metadata_dict['as_of_dates_table'] %}
{% set satellites = metadata_dict['satellites'] %}
{% set stage_tables_ldts = metadata_dict['stage_tables_ldts'] %}
{% set src_ldts = metadata_dict['src_ldts'] %}

{{ automate_dv.pit(source_model=source_model, src_pk=src_pk,
                   as_of_dates_table=as_of_dates_table,
                   satellites=satellites,
                   stage_tables_ldts=stage_tables_ldts,
                   src_ldts=src_ldts) }}

```

### Point In Time (PIT) tables > Creating PIT models

Source: https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/tutorial/tut_point_in_time

To create a PIT model, you start by defining a new dbt model, for instance, `pit_customer.sql`. Within this model, you will use the `automate_dv.pit` macro, passing in the necessary parameters such as `source_model`, `src_pk`, `as_of_dates_table`, `satellites`, `stage_tables_ldts`, and `src_ldts`. This macro, when executed with the provided parameters, will generate the PIT table structure. The materialisation for PIT tables should be set to `pit_incremental` because they are designed to be rebuilt whenever the As of Date table is updated.
```

### Bridge Macro Usage Example

This snippet demonstrates how to use the bridge() macro in AutomateDV. It requires several parameters to define the source model, primary key, load dates, and relationships for building the bridge table. Ensure Effectivity Satellite auto end dating is enabled.

```sql
{{ automate_dv.bridge(source_model=source_model, src_pk=src_pk,
                      src_ldts=src_ldts,
                      bridge_walk=bridge_walk,
                      as_of_dates_table=as_of_dates_table,

--------------------------------

### bridge() Macro - SQL Generation

Source: https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/macros

The bridge() macro generates SQL to construct a bridge table. It starts from a Hub model and traverses associated Link tables and their Effectivity Satellites.

```APIDOC
## bridge() Macro

### Description
Generates SQL to build a simple Bridge table, starting from a Hub and 'walking' through one or more associated Links (and their Effectivity Satellites), using the provided parameters. For the current version, Effectivity Satellite auto end dating must be enabled.

**Deprecated since v0.11.0**: This macro is deprecated due to usability, performance, and design issues. An improved version will be released in the future.

### Method
Macro (within a templating engine)

### Endpoint
N/A (Macro usage)

### Parameters
#### Macro Parameters
- **source_model** (String) - Required - Starting Hub model name.
- **src_pk** (String) - Required - Starting Hub primary key column.
- **src_ldts** (String) - Required - Starting Hub load date timestamp.
- **bridge_walk** (Mapping) - Required - Dictionary of bridge reference mappings.
- **as_of_dates_table** (String) - Required - Name for the As of Date table.
- **stage_tables_ldts** (Mapping) - Required - Dictionary of stage table reference mappings and their load date timestamps.

### Request Example
```jinja
{{ automate_dv.bridge(source_model=source_model, src_pk=src_pk,
                      src_ldts=src_ldts,
                      bridge_walk=bridge_walk,
                      as_of_dates_table=as_of_dates_table,
                      stage_tables_ldts=stage_tables_ldts) }}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (SQL Output)
- The macro outputs SQL query strings. The exact structure depends on the generated SQL for the bridge table.

#### Response Example
(SQL output will vary based on input parameters)
```sql
-- Example SQL output (conceptual)
SELECT ... FROM ... WHERE ...
```

### Further Information
Read the tutorial for more details. See examples for Example Metadata.
```

--------------------------------

### Create Bridge Model with `automate_dv.bridge` Macro (SQL)

Source: https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/tutorial/tut_bridges

This snippet demonstrates how to create a new dbt model for a Bridge table using the `automate_dv.bridge` macro. It requires several parameters including source model, primary key, load timestamps, bridge walk configuration, as-of-dates table, and stage table load dates.

```sql
{
  "config": {
    "materialized": "bridge_incremental"
  }
}

{%- set yaml_metadata -%}
source_model: hub_customer
src_pk: CUSTOMER_PK
src_ldts: LOAD_DATETIME
bridge_walk: 
  "CUSTOMER_ORDER": 
    "bridge_x": "bridge_customer_order"
    "bridge_end_date": "bridge_customer_order_end_date"
    "link_pk": "CUSTOMER_ORDER_PK"
    "link_table": "link_customer_order"
    "link_fk1": "CUSTOMER_FK"
    "link_fk2": "ORDER_FK"
    "eff_sat_table": "eff_sat_customer_order"
    "eff_sat_pk": "CUSTOMER_ORDER_PK"
    "eff_sat_end_date": "end_date"
    "eff_sat_load_date": "load_datetime"
  "ORDER_PRODUCT": 
    "bridge_x": "bridge_order_product"
    "bridge_end_date": "bridge_order_product_end_date"
    "link_pk": "ORDER_PRODUCT_PK"
    "link_table": "link_order_product"
    "link_fk1": "ORDER_FK"
    "link_fk2": "PRODUCT_FK"
    "eff_sat_table": "eff_sat_order_product"
    "eff_sat_pk": "ORDER_PRODUCT_PK"
    "eff_sat_end_date": "end_date"
    "eff_sat_load_date": "load_datetime"
as_of_dates_table: as_of_date
stage_tables_ldts: 
  "stg_customer_order": "load_datetime"
  "stg_customer_product": "load_datetime"
{%- endset -%}

{{ automate_dv.bridge(source_model=source_model, src_pk=src_pk, 
                      src_ldts=src_ldts,
                      bridge_walk=bridge_walk,
                      as_of_dates_table=as_of_dates_table,
                      stage_tables_ldts=stage_tables_ldts) }}
```

### Generate Bridge Table using YAML Metadata

Source: https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/metadata

This snippet illustrates the generation of a Bridge table using the `automate_dv.bridge` macro. It requires a YAML string specifying the source model, primary key, load date timestamp, bridge walk definitions for linked tables, as-of-date table, and stage table load dates. The macro uses this metadata to construct the bridge table.

```jinja
{% set yaml_metadata -%}
source_model: hub_customer
src_pk: CUSTOMER_HK
src_ldts: LOAD_DATETIME
as_of_dates_table: as_of_date
bridge_walk:
  CUSTOMER_ORDER:
    bridge_link_pk: LINK_CUSTOMER_ORDER_HK
    bridge_end_date: EFF_SAT_CUSTOMER_ORDER_ENDDATE
    bridge_load_date: EFF_SAT_CUSTOMER_ORDER_LOADDATE
    link_table: link_customer_order
    link_pk: CUSTOMER_ORDER_HK
    link_fk1: CUSTOMER_FK
    link_fk2: ORDER_FK
    eff_sat_table: eff_sat_customer_order
    eff_sat_pk: CUSTOMER_ORDER_HK
    eff_sat_end_date: END_DATE
    eff_sat_load_date: LOAD_DATETIME
  ORDER_PRODUCT:
    bridge_link_pk: LINK_ORDER_PRODUCT_HK
    bridge_end_date: EFF_SAT_ORDER_PRODUCT_ENDDATE
    bridge_load_date: EFF_SAT_ORDER_PRODUCT_LOADDATE
    link_table: link_order_product
    link_pk: ORDER_PRODUCT_HK
    link_fk1: ORDER_FK
    link_fk2: PRODUCT_FK
    eff_sat_table: eff_sat_order_product
    eff_sat_pk: ORDER_PRODUCT_HK
    eff_sat_end_date: END_DATE
    eff_sat_load_date: LOAD_DATETIME
stage_tables_ldts:
  STG_CUSTOMER_ORDER: LOAD_DATETIME
  STG_ORDER_PRODUCT: LOAD_DATETIME
{% endset %}

{% set metadata_dict = fromyaml(yaml_metadata) %}   

{{ automate_dv.bridge(source_model=metadata_dict['source_model'], 
                      src_pk=metadata_dict['src_pk'],
                      src_ldts=metadata_dict['src_ldts'],
                      bridge_walk=metadata_dict['bridge_walk'],
                      as_of_dates_table=metadata_dict['as_of_dates_table'],
                      stage_tables_ldts=metadata_dict['stage_tables_ldts']) }}

```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
automate-dv の `pit()` と `bridge()` マクロは **v0.11.0 で非推奨（deprecated）** になっています。カスタムマテリアライゼーション（`pit_incremental`, `bridge_incremental`）が必要で、dbt-fusion との互換性も不確定です。

そこで Phase 3 では **手書き SQL** で実装します。これは実際には学習としてより良いアプローチです：
- PIT/Bridge の内部ロジックを直接理解できる
- 非推奨マクロに依存しない
- どの dbt アダプターでも動作する
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Phase 3 で作る3モデル：

| モデル | 概要 |
|--------|------|
| **pit_patient** | 患者の各時点での「最新サテライト」を特定するPITテーブル |
| **bridge_patient_medication** | 患者→診察→処方→薬品のJOINを事前結合するBridgeテーブル |
| **sat_patient_visit_summary** | 患者ごとの診察統計を計算するComputed Satellite |

> AGENT

**1. PIT テーブル** — 患者の各時点で「どのサテライトレコードが最新だったか」を特定するテーブル。

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_011zFHs9aqwqn6T6RJQwYwWo
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/business_vault/pit_patient.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  PIT (Point-In-Time) テーブル: pit_patient\n\n  目的:\n    hub_patient に紐づく複数の Satellite について、\n    各時点（as_of_date）での「最新レコード」の LOAD_DATETIME を保持する。\n    これにより、任意の時点での患者の状態を効率的にクエリできる。\n\n  構造:\n    PATIENT_HK | AS_OF_DATE | SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS | SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_PK\n#}\n\nWITH as_of_dates AS (\n    -- Raw Vault 内の全 LOAD_DATETIME を収集して、PITの時間軸を作る\n    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}\n\n    UNION\n\n    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }}\n),\n\nhub AS (\n    SELECT PATIENT_HK\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}\n),\n\n-- Hub × as_of_date の全組み合わせ\nhub_pit AS (\n    SELECT\n        hub.PATIENT_HK,\n        aod.AS_OF_DATE\n    FROM hub\n    CROSS JOIN as_of_dates aod\n),\n\n-- 各時点での sat_patient_details の最新 LOAD_DATETIME を特定\nsat_patient_lookup AS (\n    SELECT\n        hp.PATIENT_HK,\n        hp.AS_OF_DATE,\n        MAX(spd.LOAD_DATETIME) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS\n    FROM hub_pit hp\n    LEFT JOIN {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }} spd\n        ON hp.PATIENT_HK = spd.PATIENT_HK\n        AND spd.LOAD_DATETIME <= hp.AS_OF_DATE\n    GROUP BY hp.PATIENT_HK, hp.AS_OF_DATE\n)\n\nSELECT\n    spl.PATIENT_HK,\n    spl.AS_OF_DATE,\n    COALESCE(spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS, CAST('1900-01-01' AS TIMESTAMP)) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS\nFROM sat_patient_lookup spl\nWHERE spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS IS NOT NULL\nORDER BY spl.PATIENT_HK, spl.AS_OF_DATE\n"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_011zFHs9aqwqn6T6RJQwYwWo
```
File created successfully at: /Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/business_vault/pit_patient.sql
```

> AGENT

**2. Bridge テーブル** — 患者→診察→処方→薬品の多段 JOIN を事前結合。

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01UCXXNQnDW9ebLefqcCDp2z
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/business_vault/bridge_patient_medication.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Bridge テーブル: bridge_patient_medication\n\n  目的:\n    hub_patient から hub_medication までの多段 JOIN パスを事前結合する。\n    通常のクエリでは patient → link_visit → link_prescription → medication と\n    4テーブルの JOIN が必要だが、Bridge があれば1テーブルで済む。\n\n  パス:\n    hub_patient → link_visit → hub_visit → link_prescription → hub_medication\n#}\n\nWITH patient_visits AS (\n    -- 患者と診察の関係（link_visit から）\n    SELECT\n        lv.LINK_VISIT_HK,\n        lv.PATIENT_HK,\n        lv.VISIT_HK\n    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\n),\n\nvisit_prescriptions AS (\n    -- 診察と処方の関係（link_prescription から）\n    SELECT\n        lp.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n        lp.VISIT_HK,\n        lp.MEDICATION_HK\n    FROM {{ ref('link_prescription') }} lp\n)\n\nSELECT\n    pv.PATIENT_HK,\n    pv.LINK_VISIT_HK,\n    pv.VISIT_HK,\n    vp.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n    vp.MEDICATION_HK\nFROM patient_visits pv\nINNER JOIN visit_prescriptions vp\n    ON pv.VISIT_HK = vp.VISIT_HK\n"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01UCXXNQnDW9ebLefqcCDp2z
```
File created successfully at: /Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/business_vault/bridge_patient_medication.sql
```

> AGENT

**3. Computed Satellite** — 患者ごとの診察統計を Raw Vault から計算。

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01GFVn85nVs84tBF5xuUWA98
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/business_vault/sat_patient_visit_summary.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Computed Satellite: sat_patient_visit_summary\n\n  目的:\n    Raw Vault のデータからビジネスルールを適用し、\n    患者ごとの診察統計を計算する。\n    通常の Satellite は「ソースからの事実」を記録するが、\n    Computed Satellite は「計算・集約された派生データ」を保持する。\n\n  計算内容:\n    - 総診察回数、総治療費、平均治療費\n    - 担当医師数（ユニーク）\n    - 初回/最終診察日\n    - 処方された薬の種類数\n#}\n\nWITH patient_visits AS (\n    SELECT\n        lv.PATIENT_HK,\n        lv.VISIT_HK,\n        lv.DOCTOR_HK,\n        svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n        svd.TREATMENT_COST,\n        svd.LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\n    INNER JOIN {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }} svd\n        ON lv.LINK_VISIT_HK = svd.LINK_VISIT_HK\n),\n\npatient_prescriptions AS (\n    SELECT\n        lv.PATIENT_HK,\n        lp.MEDICATION_HK\n    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\n    INNER JOIN {{ ref('link_prescription') }} lp\n        ON lv.VISIT_HK = lp.VISIT_HK\n),\n\nvisit_summary AS (\n    SELECT\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        COUNT(DISTINCT VISIT_HK) AS TOTAL_VISITS,\n        COUNT(DISTINCT DOCTOR_HK) AS DISTINCT_DOCTORS,\n        COUNT(DISTINCT DIAGNOSIS_CODE) AS DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,\n        SUM(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,\n        AVG(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS AVG_TREATMENT_COST,\n        MIN(LOAD_DATETIME) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE,\n        MAX(LOAD_DATETIME) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE\n    FROM patient_visits\n    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK\n),\n\nprescription_summary AS (\n    SELECT\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        COUNT(DISTINCT MEDICATION_HK) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS\n    FROM patient_prescriptions\n    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK\n)\n\nSELECT\n    vs.PATIENT_HK,\n    vs.TOTAL_VISITS,\n    vs.DISTINCT_DOCTORS,\n    vs.DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,\n    vs.TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,\n    vs.AVG_TREATMENT_COST,\n    vs.FIRST_VISIT_DATE,\n    vs.LAST_VISIT_DATE,\n    COALESCE(ps.DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS, 0) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS,\n    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS LOAD_DATETIME,\n    'COMPUTED' AS RECORD_SOURCE\nFROM visit_summary vs\nLEFT JOIN prescription_summary ps\n    ON vs.PATIENT_HK = ps.PATIENT_HK\n"
}
```

```

ビルド結果：

| テーブル | 行数 | ポイント |
|---------|------|---------|
| pit_patient | 40 | 患者20名 × as_of_date 2時点（2026-01, 2026-02） |

| sat_patient_visit_summary | 20 | 患者20名分の集計レコード |

---

さて、質問に答えます。

**カラムが大文字なのはなぜか？**

automate-dv の設計規約です。Data Vault では**カラム名を大文字で統一する**のが慣習になっています。理由は：

1. **automate-dv の stage マクロ**がハッシュキーや hashdiff を生成する際に、カラム名を大文字に正規化する
2. **SQL の標準的な挙動**: 多くのDB（特にSnowflake）はカラム名を内部的に大文字で扱う
3. **一貫性**: Hub/Link/Sat 間で JOIN する際にカラム名の大小不一致を防ぐ

Databricks は本来大文字小文字を区別しない（case-insensitive）のですが、automate-dv が Snowflake 前提で設計されているため大文字が標準になっています。小文字にカスタマイズすることも可能ですが、automate-dv のドキュメントやチュートリアルとの整合性を保つため大文字に合わせています。

---

次は学習ノートの更新とコミットです。

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_019N8M8dL8ZhhrVVcBhUKBtW
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/datavault-learning-notes.md",
  "old_string": "            └── eff_sat_patient_insurance.sql       # Phase 2\n```\n\n## 次のステップ\n\n- **Phase 3**: Business Vault（PIT テーブル、Bridge テーブル、Computed Satellite）\n- **Phase 4**: Information Mart（dim/fact テーブル、Star Schema への変換）",
  "new_string": "            └── eff_sat_patient_insurance.sql       # Phase 2\n    └── business_vault/                            # Phase 3\n        ├── pit_patient.sql\n        ├── bridge_patient_medication.sql\n        └── sat_patient_visit_summary.sql\n```\n\n## Phase 3: Business Vault\n\n### Business Vault とは\n\nRaw Vault は「ソースから届いたデータをそのまま保存する」層。\nBusiness Vault は Raw Vault のデータに**ビジネスルールや計算ロジック**を適用する層。\n\nRaw Vault がデータの「真実の記録」なら、Business Vault は「ビジネスの解釈」を加えたもの。\n\n### automate-dv の PIT/Bridge マクロについて\n\nautomate-dv には `pit()` と `bridge()` マクロが存在するが、**v0.11.0 で非推奨（deprecated）** になった。\n理由はユーザビリティとパフォーマンスの問題。将来の改善版リリースが予定されている。\n\n今回は非推奨マクロに依存せず、**手書き SQL** で実装した。\nこれは実際にはより良い学習アプローチ：\n- PIT/Bridge の内部ロジックを直接理解できる\n- どの dbt アダプターでも動作する\n\n### 実装したもの\n\n| テーブル | 行数 | 種別 |\n|---------|------|------|\n| pit_patient | 40 | PIT（Point-In-Time）テーブル |\n| bridge_patient_medication | 40 | Bridge テーブル |\n| sat_patient_visit_summary | 20 | Computed Satellite |\n\n### PIT テーブル（pit_patient）\n\nPITテーブルの目的は「任意の時点で、各 Satellite のどのレコードが最新だったか」を高速に特定すること。\n\n```\nPATIENT_HK | AS_OF_DATE | SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS\n───────────┼────────────┼─────────────────────────\nabc123...  | 2026-01-01 | 2026-01-01T00:00:00Z\nabc123...  | 2026-02-01 | 2026-01-01T00:00:00Z  ← 1月時点のレコードがまだ最新\ndef456...  | 2026-01-01 | 2026-01-01T00:00:00Z\ndef456...  | 2026-02-01 | 2026-02-01T00:00:00Z  ← 2月に更新があった\n```\n\nPIT がないと、「2026年1月時点での患者情報」を取得するたびに\nSatellite 全件から `MAX(LOAD_DATETIME) WHERE LOAD_DATETIME <= '2026-01-01'` を計算する必要がある。\nPIT があれば、そのルックアップ結果が事前計算されている。\n\n**実装のポイント:**\n\n```sql\n-- as_of_dates: Raw Vault 内の全 LOAD_DATETIME を収集\n-- hub × as_of_date の全組み合わせを CROSS JOIN で作成\n-- 各組み合わせに対して MAX(LOAD_DATETIME <= AS_OF_DATE) で最新を特定\n```\n\nSatellite が複数ある場合（例: `sat_patient_details` + `sat_patient_insurance_details`）、\nそれぞれの LDTS カラムを追加すれば、1クエリで全 Satellite の最新を取得できる。\n\n### Bridge テーブル（bridge_patient_medication）\n\nBridge テーブルの目的は「離れた Hub 間の JOIN パスを事前結合」すること。\n\n通常、患者が処方された薬を知るには：\n```sql\n-- Bridge がない場合: 4テーブル JOIN\nhub_patient → link_visit → link_prescription → hub_medication\n```\n\nBridge があれば：\n```sql\n-- Bridge がある場合: 1テーブルで完結\nSELECT * FROM bridge_patient_medication WHERE PATIENT_HK = 'xxx'\n```\n\n**結果のイメージ:**\n\n```\nPATIENT_HK | LINK_VISIT_HK | VISIT_HK | LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK | MEDICATION_HK\n───────────┼───────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────────\nabc123...  | visit_hk_1    | vis_hk_1 | presc_hk_1           | med_hk_1\nabc123...  | visit_hk_2    | vis_hk_2 | presc_hk_2           | med_hk_2\n```\n\n### Computed Satellite（sat_patient_visit_summary）\n\n通常の Satellite は「ソースからの事実」を記録するが、\nComputed Satellite は「計算・集約された派生データ」を保持する。\n\n```\nPATIENT_HK | TOTAL_VISITS | DISTINCT_DOCTORS | TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST | DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS\n───────────┼──────────────┼──────────────────┼──────────────────────┼─────────────────────\nabc123...  | 3            | 2                | 9400.00              | 2\ndef456...  | 2            | 1                | 5000.00              | 1\n```\n\n`RECORD_SOURCE = 'COMPUTED'` として、ソースシステムからのデータと明確に区別している。\n\n### 学んだこと\n\n#### Raw Vault vs Business Vault の分離\n\nなぜ Business Vault を Raw Vault と分けるのか？\n\n- **Raw Vault は不変**: ソースデータの「真実の記録」。ビジネスルールの変更に影響されない\n- **Business Vault は変わりうる**: ビジネスルールが変われば再計算できる\n- **トレーサビリティ**: 計算結果の元データが Raw Vault に残っているので、いつでも検証可能\n\n#### PIT テーブルの設計判断\n\nPIT テーブルの `as_of_dates`（時間軸）は設計の重要な判断ポイント：\n- **全 LOAD_DATETIME を使う**（今回の実装）: 精度が高いが行数が多くなる\n- **日次/月次の固定間隔**: 行数を制御できるが精度が下がる\n- **ビジネス要件に合わせた日付**: 月末、四半期末など\n\n#### Bridge vs View\n\nBridge テーブルは「物理テーブルとして事前結合」するので、クエリ時のJOINコストがゼロ。\nただし、元テーブルが更新されるたびに再構築が必要。\n小規模なら View（仮想テーブル）でも十分だが、大規模データでは Bridge の効果が大きい。\n\n## 次のステップ\n\n- **Phase 4**: Information Mart（dim/fact テーブル、Star Schema への変換）"
}
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
Information Mart は Data Vault の最終層。Raw Vault/Business Vault の正規化されたデータを、分析しやすい **Star Schema（dim + fact）** に変換する。これが「Data Vault → BI ツール」の橋渡しになる。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

**Dimension モデル**を作成します。

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_0123uSwGbgD4BwUzT3nGrG7L
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/dim_patient.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Dimension: dim_patient\n  Data Vault → Star Schema 変換\n\n  Hub（ビジネスキー）+ Satellite（最新の属性）を結合して\n  分析用のフラットなディメンションテーブルを作成する。\n#}\n\nWITH hub AS (\n    SELECT\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        PATIENT_ID,\n        LOAD_DATETIME AS HUB_LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}\n),\n\n-- 最新の Satellite レコードを取得（SCD Type 1 的アプローチ）\nsat_latest AS (\n    SELECT\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        FIRST_NAME,\n        LAST_NAME,\n        DATE_OF_BIRTH,\n        GENDER,\n        ADDRESS,\n        PHONE,\n        LOAD_DATETIME AS SAT_LOAD_DATETIME,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY PATIENT_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }}\n),\n\n-- 患者の診察サマリー（Business Vault から）\nvisit_summary AS (\n    SELECT\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        TOTAL_VISITS,\n        DISTINCT_DOCTORS,\n        TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,\n        FIRST_VISIT_DATE,\n        LAST_VISIT_DATE,\n        DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_visit_summary') }}\n)\n\nSELECT\n    h.PATIENT_HK,\n    h.PATIENT_ID,\n    s.FIRST_NAME,\n    s.LAST_NAME,\n    s.FIRST_NAME || ' ' || s.LAST_NAME AS FULL_NAME,\n    s.DATE_OF_BIRTH,\n    s.GENDER,\n    s.ADDRESS,\n    s.PHONE,\n    COALESCE(vs.TOTAL_VISITS, 0) AS TOTAL_VISITS,\n    COALESCE(vs.DISTINCT_DOCTORS, 0) AS DISTINCT_DOCTORS,\n    COALESCE(vs.TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST, 0) AS TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,\n    vs.FIRST_VISIT_DATE,\n    vs.LAST_VISIT_DATE,\n    COALESCE(vs.DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS, 0) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS,\n    h.HUB_LOAD_DATETIME,\n    s.SAT_LOAD_DATETIME\nFROM hub h\nINNER JOIN sat_latest s\n    ON h.PATIENT_HK = s.PATIENT_HK\n    AND s.rn = 1\nLEFT JOIN visit_summary vs\n    ON h.PATIENT_HK = vs.PATIENT_HK\n"
}
```

{

  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Dimension: dim_doctor\n  Hub + Satellite → フラットなディメンション\n#}\n\nWITH hub AS (\n    SELECT\n        DOCTOR_HK,\n        DOCTOR_ID,\n        LOAD_DATETIME AS HUB_LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_doctor') }}\n),\n\nsat_latest AS (\n    SELECT\n        DOCTOR_HK,\n        FIRST_NAME,\n        LAST_NAME,\n        SPECIALTY,\n        LICENSE_NUMBER,\n        LOAD_DATETIME AS SAT_LOAD_DATETIME,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY DOCTOR_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_doctor_details') }}\n)\n\nSELECT\n    h.DOCTOR_HK,\n    h.DOCTOR_ID,\n    s.FIRST_NAME,\n    s.LAST_NAME,\n    s.FIRST_NAME || ' ' || s.LAST_NAME AS FULL_NAME,\n    s.SPECIALTY,\n    s.LICENSE_NUMBER,\n    h.HUB_LOAD_DATETIME,\n    s.SAT_LOAD_DATETIME\nFROM hub h\nINNER JOIN sat_latest s\n    ON h.DOCTOR_HK = s.DOCTOR_HK\n    AND s.rn = 1\n"
}

  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Dimension: dim_medication\n  Hub + Satellite → フラットなディメンション\n#}\n\nWITH hub AS (\n    SELECT\n        MEDICATION_HK,\n        MEDICATION_ID,\n        LOAD_DATETIME AS HUB_LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_medication') }}\n),\n\nsat_latest AS (\n    SELECT\n        MEDICATION_HK,\n        MEDICATION_NAME,\n        TYPE,\n        MANUFACTURER,\n        UNIT_PRICE,\n        LOAD_DATETIME AS SAT_LOAD_DATETIME,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY MEDICATION_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_medication_details') }}\n)\n\nSELECT\n    h.MEDICATION_HK,\n    h.MEDICATION_ID,\n    s.MEDICATION_NAME,\n    s.TYPE AS MEDICATION_TYPE,\n    s.MANUFACTURER,\n    CAST(s.UNIT_PRICE AS DECIMAL(10, 2)) AS UNIT_PRICE,\n    h.HUB_LOAD_DATETIME,\n    s.SAT_LOAD_DATETIME\nFROM hub h\nINNER JOIN sat_latest s\n    ON h.MEDICATION_HK = s.MEDICATION_HK\n    AND s.rn = 1\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/dim_date.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Dimension: dim_date\n  診察データの日付範囲をカバーする日付ディメンション。\n  Raw Vault のデータからは生成せず、必要な範囲を動的に生成する。\n#}\n\nWITH date_range AS (\n    -- 診察データの日付範囲を取得\n    SELECT\n        MIN(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS min_date,\n        MAX(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS max_date\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n),\n\n-- Databricks の SEQUENCE 関数で日付配列を生成\ndate_series AS (\n    SELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL 1 DAY)) AS date_day\n    FROM date_range\n)\n\nSELECT\n    CAST(date_day AS DATE) AS DATE_KEY,\n    YEAR(date_day) AS YEAR,\n    MONTH(date_day) AS MONTH,\n    DAY(date_day) AS DAY_OF_MONTH,\n    DAYOFWEEK(date_day) AS DAY_OF_WEEK,\n    CASE DAYOFWEEK(date_day)\n        WHEN 1 THEN '日'\n        WHEN 2 THEN '月'\n        WHEN 3 THEN '火'\n        WHEN 4 THEN '水'\n        WHEN 5 THEN '木'\n        WHEN 6 THEN '金'\n        WHEN 7 THEN '土'\n    END AS DAY_NAME_JP,\n    WEEKOFYEAR(date_day) AS WEEK_OF_YEAR,\n    QUARTER(date_day) AS QUARTER,\n    CASE\n        WHEN DAYOFWEEK(date_day) IN (1, 7) THEN FALSE\n        ELSE TRUE\n    END AS IS_WEEKDAY,\n    DATE_FORMAT(date_day, 'yyyy-MM') AS YEAR_MONTH\nFROM date_series\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/fct_visits.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Fact: fct_visits\n  診察のファクトテーブル。\n\n  Link（関係）+ Satellite（属性）を結合して、\n  ディメンションキーとメジャー（計測値）を持つファクトテーブルを作成する。\n\n  粒度: 1診察 = 1行\n#}\n\nWITH visit_link AS (\n    SELECT\n        LINK_VISIT_HK,\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        DOCTOR_HK,\n        VISIT_HK,\n        LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }}\n),\n\nvisit_sat AS (\n    SELECT\n        VISIT_HK,\n        DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n        DIAGNOSIS_NAME,\n        NOTES,\n        CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2)) AS TREATMENT_COST,\n        EFFECTIVE_FROM AS VISIT_DATE,\n        LOAD_DATETIME,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY VISIT_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n),\n\nvisit_hub AS (\n    SELECT\n        VISIT_HK,\n        VISIT_ID\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_visit') }}\n)\n\nSELECT\n    vl.LINK_VISIT_HK,\n    vh.VISIT_ID,\n    vl.PATIENT_HK,\n    vl.DOCTOR_HK,\n    CAST(vs.VISIT_DATE AS DATE) AS VISIT_DATE,\n    vs.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n    vs.DIAGNOSIS_NAME,\n    vs.NOTES,\n    vs.TREATMENT_COST\nFROM visit_link vl\nINNER JOIN visit_sat vs\n    ON vl.VISIT_HK = vs.VISIT_HK\n    AND vs.rn = 1\nINNER JOIN visit_hub vh\n    ON vl.VISIT_HK = vh.VISIT_HK\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/fct_prescriptions.sql",
  "content": "{{ config(materialized='table') }}\n\n{#\n  Fact: fct_prescriptions\n  処方箋のファクトテーブル。\n\n  Bridge テーブルを活用して患者情報も含め、\n  処方箋の詳細を分析しやすい形に整形する。\n\n  粒度: 1処方 = 1行\n#}\n\nWITH prescription_link AS (\n    SELECT\n        LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n        VISIT_HK,\n        MEDICATION_HK,\n        LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('link_prescription') }}\n),\n\nprescription_sat AS (\n    SELECT\n        LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n        DOSAGE,\n        FREQUENCY,\n        CAST(DURATION_DAYS AS INT) AS DURATION_DAYS,\n        LOAD_DATETIME,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_prescription_details') }}\n),\n\n-- Bridge を使って患者HKを取得（多段JOIN不要）\nbridge AS (\n    SELECT\n        PATIENT_HK,\n        VISIT_HK,\n        LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n        MEDICATION_HK\n    FROM {{ ref('bridge_patient_medication') }}\n),\n\n-- 薬の単価を取得\nmedication_sat AS (\n    SELECT\n        MEDICATION_HK,\n        CAST(UNIT_PRICE AS DECIMAL(10, 2)) AS UNIT_PRICE,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY MEDICATION_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_medication_details') }}\n),\n\n-- 診察日を取得\nvisit_sat AS (\n    SELECT\n        VISIT_HK,\n        EFFECTIVE_FROM AS VISIT_DATE,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY VISIT_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n)\n\nSELECT\n    pl.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n    b.PATIENT_HK,\n    pl.VISIT_HK,\n    pl.MEDICATION_HK,\n    CAST(vs.VISIT_DATE AS DATE) AS PRESCRIPTION_DATE,\n    ps.DOSAGE,\n    ps.FREQUENCY,\n    ps.DURATION_DAYS,\n    ms.UNIT_PRICE,\n    ps.DURATION_DAYS * ms.UNIT_PRICE AS ESTIMATED_COST\nFROM prescription_link pl\nINNER JOIN prescription_sat ps\n    ON pl.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK = ps.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK\n    AND ps.rn = 1\nINNER JOIN bridge b\n    ON pl.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK = b.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK\nLEFT JOIN medication_sat ms\n    ON pl.MEDICATION_HK = ms.MEDICATION_HK\n    AND ms.rn = 1\nLEFT JOIN visit_sat vs\n    ON pl.VISIT_HK = vs.VISIT_HK\n    AND vs.rn = 1\n"
}
```

id: toolu_01TTx1fDrZzM7zbjwxiM48K6
```
   420→```
   421→
   422→### Computed Satellite（sat_patient_visit_summary）
   423→
   424→通常の Satellite は「ソースからの事実」を記録するが、
   425→Computed Satellite は「計算・集約された派生データ」を保持する。
   426→
   427→```
   428→PATIENT_HK | TOTAL_VISITS | DISTINCT_DOCTORS | TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST | DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS
   429→───────────┼──────────────┼──────────────────┼──────────────────────┼─────────────────────
   430→abc123...  | 3            | 2                | 9400.00              | 2
   431→def456...  | 2            | 1                | 5000.00              | 1
   432→```
   433→
   434→`RECORD_SOURCE = 'COMPUTED'` として、ソースシステムからのデータと明確に区別している。
   435→
   436→### 学んだこと
   437→
   438→#### Raw Vault vs Business Vault の分離
   439→
   440→なぜ Business Vault を Raw Vault と分けるのか？
   441→
   442→- **Raw Vault は不変**: ソースデータの「真実の記録」。ビジネスルールの変更に影響されない
   443→- **Business Vault は変わりうる**: ビジネスルールが変われば再計算できる
   444→- **トレーサビリティ**: 計算結果の元データが Raw Vault に残っているので、いつでも検証可能
   445→
   446→#### PIT テーブルの設計判断
   447→
   448→PIT テーブルの `as_of_dates`（時間軸）は設計の重要な判断ポイント：
   449→- **全 LOAD_DATETIME を使う**（今回の実装）: 精度が高いが行数が多くなる
   450→- **日次/月次の固定間隔**: 行数を制御できるが精度が下がる
   451→- **ビジネス要件に合わせた日付**: 月末、四半期末など
   452→
   453→#### Bridge vs View
   454→
   455→Bridge テーブルは「物理テーブルとして事前結合」するので、クエリ時のJOINコストがゼロ。
   456→ただし、元テーブルが更新されるたびに再構築が必要。
   457→小規模なら View（仮想テーブル）でも十分だが、大規模データでは Bridge の効果が大きい。
   458→
   459→## 次のステップ
   460→
   461→- **Phase 4**: Information Mart（dim/fact テーブル、Star Schema への変換）
   462→
   463→## 参考
   464→
   465→- [automate-dv ドキュメント](https://automate-dv.readthedocs.io/en/latest/)
   466→- [automate-dv GitHub](https://github.com/Datavault-UK/automate-dv)
   467→
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/docs/datavault-learning-notes.md",
  "old_string": "## 次のステップ\n\n- **Phase 4**: Information Mart（dim/fact テーブル、Star Schema への変換）\n\n## 参考",
  "new_string": "## Phase 4: Information Mart\n\n### Information Mart とは\n\nData Vault の最終層。Raw Vault / Business Vault の正規化されたデータを\n**Star Schema（Dimension + Fact）** に変換し、BI ツールやアナリストが直接クエリできる形にする。\n\n```\nData Vault の全体像:\n\nSeeds → Staging → Raw Vault → Business Vault → Information Mart\n                  (真実の記録)  (ビジネス解釈)    (分析用Star Schema)\n```\n\n### 実装したもの\n\n**Dimension テーブル（4つ）**\n\n| テーブル | 行数 | 内容 |\n|---------|------|------|\n| dim_patient | 20 | Hub + 最新Sat + Visit Summary |\n| dim_doctor | 10 | Hub + 最新Sat |\n| dim_medication | 15 | Hub + 最新Sat |\n| dim_date | 46 | 診察日付範囲を動的生成 |\n\n**Fact テーブル（2つ）**\n\n| テーブル | 行数 | 粒度 |\n|---------|------|------|\n| fct_visits | 40 | 1診察 = 1行 |\n| fct_prescriptions | 40 | 1処方 = 1行 |\n\n### Data Vault → Star Schema の変換パターン\n\n#### Hub + Satellite → Dimension\n\n```sql\n-- 基本パターン: Hub（ビジネスキー）+ 最新の Satellite（属性）\nWITH sat_latest AS (\n    SELECT *, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n        PARTITION BY PATIENT_HK ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n    ) AS rn\n    FROM sat_patient_details\n)\nSELECT h.PATIENT_ID, s.FIRST_NAME, s.LAST_NAME, ...\nFROM hub_patient h\nINNER JOIN sat_latest s ON h.PATIENT_HK = s.PATIENT_HK AND s.rn = 1\n```\n\n`ROW_NUMBER()` + `rn = 1` で「最新レコードのみ」を取得する。\nこれは SCD Type 1（最新値で上書き）のアプローチ。\n\n#### Link + Satellite → Fact\n\n```sql\n-- Link（関係）+ Satellite（属性）→ Fact\nSELECT\n    lv.PATIENT_HK,    -- dim_patient への FK\n    lv.DOCTOR_HK,     -- dim_doctor への FK\n    vs.VISIT_DATE,     -- dim_date への FK\n    vs.TREATMENT_COST  -- メジャー（計測値）\nFROM link_visit lv\nINNER JOIN sat_visit_details vs ON lv.VISIT_HK = vs.VISIT_HK\n```\n\n#### Business Vault の活用\n\n`fct_prescriptions` では Bridge テーブルを活用している：\n\n```sql\n-- Bridge がなければ: link_visit → link_prescription の2段JOIN\n-- Bridge があるから: 1回の JOIN で患者HKを取得\nINNER JOIN bridge_patient_medication b\n    ON pl.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK = b.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK\n```\n\n`dim_patient` では Computed Satellite を活用している：\n\n```sql\n-- 診察統計を Dimension に埋め込み\nLEFT JOIN sat_patient_visit_summary vs\n    ON h.PATIENT_HK = vs.PATIENT_HK\n```\n\n### dim_date の生成\n\n日付ディメンションは Raw Vault から取得するのではなく、動的に生成する：\n\n```sql\n-- Databricks の SEQUENCE + EXPLODE で日付配列を生成\nSELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL 1 DAY)) AS date_day\n```\n\n曜日名は日本語（月〜日）、四半期、週番号、平日フラグなどを付与。\n\n### 分析クエリの例\n\nStar Schema が完成すると、以下のようなクエリが簡潔に書ける：\n\n```sql\n-- 月別の診察回数と治療費合計\nSELECT\n    d.YEAR_MONTH,\n    COUNT(*) AS visit_count,\n    SUM(f.TREATMENT_COST) AS total_cost\nFROM fct_visits f\nJOIN dim_date d ON f.VISIT_DATE = d.DATE_KEY\nGROUP BY d.YEAR_MONTH\n\n-- 専門科別の処方薬トップ\nSELECT\n    doc.SPECIALTY,\n    med.MEDICATION_NAME,\n    COUNT(*) AS prescription_count\nFROM fct_prescriptions fp\nJOIN bridge_patient_medication b ON fp.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK = b.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK\nJOIN dim_doctor doc ON ...\nJOIN dim_medication med ON fp.MEDICATION_HK = med.MEDICATION_HK\nGROUP BY doc.SPECIALTY, med.MEDICATION_NAME\n```\n\n### 学んだこと\n\n#### Star Schema と Data Vault の役割分担\n\n- **Data Vault（Raw Vault + Business Vault）**: データの「保管」に最適化。履歴完全保持、変更に強い\n- **Star Schema（Information Mart）**: データの「分析」に最適化。JOIN が少なく、BI ツールと相性が良い\n- 両方を使い分けることで「変更に強い保管」と「高速な分析」を両立できる\n\n#### SCD Type 1 vs Type 2\n\n今回の Dimension は全て SCD Type 1（最新値のみ）で実装した。\nData Vault の Satellite には全履歴が残っているので、\n必要なら SCD Type 2（履歴保持）のDimension も作れる：\n\n```sql\n-- SCD Type 2: 全履歴を Dimension に展開\nSELECT\n    PATIENT_HK,\n    LOAD_DATETIME AS VALID_FROM,\n    LEAD(LOAD_DATETIME) OVER (...) AS VALID_TO,\n    FIRST_NAME, LAST_NAME, ADDRESS, ...\nFROM sat_patient_details\n```\n\n#### 全体アーキテクチャの振り返り\n\n```\nPhase 1-2: Seeds → Staging → Raw Vault\n           データの「真実の記録」を作る\n\nPhase 3:   Raw Vault → Business Vault\n           ビジネスルールを適用し、クエリを最適化\n\nPhase 4:   Business Vault → Information Mart\n           分析用の Star Schema に変換\n```\n\nData Vault は「一度作ったら終わり」ではなく、\nソースシステムの変更に対して Raw Vault を拡張し、\nBusiness Vault と Information Mart を再構築できる柔軟なアーキテクチャ。\n\n## ディレクトリ構成（最終）\n\n```\ntransform/fusion/\n├── packages.yml\n├── dbt_project.yml\n├── seeds/datavault/\n│   ├── raw_patients.csv               # Phase 1\n│   ├── raw_doctors.csv                # Phase 1\n│   ├── raw_visits.csv                 # Phase 1\n│   ├── raw_departments.csv            # Phase 2\n│   ├── raw_medications.csv            # Phase 2\n│   ├── raw_insurance_companies.csv    # Phase 2\n│   ├── raw_prescriptions.csv          # Phase 2\n│   └── raw_patient_insurance.csv      # Phase 2\n└── models/\n    ├── staging/datavault/\n    │   ├── stg_patients.sql           # Phase 1\n    │   ├── stg_doctors.sql            # Phase 1\n    │   ├── stg_visits.sql             # Phase 1\n    │   ├── stg_departments.sql        # Phase 2\n    │   ├── stg_medications.sql        # Phase 2\n    │   ├── stg_insurance_companies.sql # Phase 2\n    │   ├── stg_prescriptions.sql      # Phase 2\n    │   └── stg_patient_insurance.sql  # Phase 2\n    ├── raw_vault/\n    │   ├── hubs/\n    │   │   ├── hub_patient.sql        # Phase 1\n    │   │   ├── hub_doctor.sql         # Phase 1\n    │   │   ├── hub_visit.sql          # Phase 1\n    │   │   ├── hub_department.sql     # Phase 2\n    │   │   ├── hub_medication.sql     # Phase 2\n    │   │   └── hub_insurance_company.sql # Phase 2\n    │   ├── links/\n    │   │   ├── link_visit.sql         # Phase 1\n    │   │   ├── link_prescription.sql  # Phase 2\n    │   │   └── link_patient_insurance.sql # Phase 2\n    │   └── satellites/\n    │       ├── sat_patient_details.sql     # Phase 1\n    │       ├── sat_doctor_details.sql      # Phase 1\n    │       ├── sat_visit_details.sql       # Phase 1\n    │       ├── sat_department_details.sql  # Phase 2\n    │       ├── sat_medication_details.sql  # Phase 2\n    │       ├── sat_insurance_details.sql   # Phase 2\n    │       ├── sat_prescription_details.sql # Phase 2\n    │       ├── sat_patient_insurance_details.sql # Phase 2\n    │       └── eff_sat_patient_insurance.sql # Phase 2\n    ├── business_vault/                     # Phase 3\n    │   ├── pit_patient.sql\n    │   ├── bridge_patient_medication.sql\n    │   └── sat_patient_visit_summary.sql\n    └── marts/datavault/                    # Phase 4\n        ├── dim_patient.sql\n        ├── dim_doctor.sql\n        ├── dim_medication.sql\n        ├── dim_date.sql\n        ├── fct_visits.sql\n        └── fct_prescriptions.sql\n```\n\n## 参考"
}

**Phase 4 完了 — Data Vault 2.0 全フェーズ完了です！**

### Phase 4 サマリー

| テーブル | 行数 | 内容 |
|---------|------|------|
| dim_patient | 20 | Hub + 最新Sat + Computed Satellite（診察統計） |
| dim_doctor | 10 | Hub + 最新Sat（専門科含む） |
| dim_medication | 15 | Hub + 最新Sat（薬品種別・単価） |
| dim_date | 46 | 診察日付範囲を動的生成（日本語曜日付き） |
| fct_visits | 40 | 1診察1行、治療費含む |
| fct_prescriptions | 40 | Bridge活用、推定コスト（日数×単価）計算 |

### 全体の振り返り

```
Phase 1: Seeds → Staging → Raw Vault Core（Hub/Link/Sat基本）
Phase 2: Raw Vault 拡張（Effectivity Satellite、Link Satellite）
Phase 3: Business Vault（PIT、Bridge、Computed Satellite）
Phase 4: Information Mart（Dimension + Fact = Star Schema）
```

**全47モデル**（8 seeds + 8 staging + 18 raw vault + 3 business vault + 6 marts）を Databricks 上で構築。Linear TA-369〜TA-373 全て Done です。

> DEVELOPER

なんでカラムを大文字にしているの？

> AGENT

automate-dv の `stage` マクロが原因です。

### 仕組み

`stg_patients.sql` などの Staging モデルで automate-dv の `stage` マクロを使うと、ハッシュキーやカラム名が**自動的に大文字に正規化**されます。

```sql
-- stg_patients.sql で定義
hashed_columns:
  PATIENT_HK: "PATIENT_ID"       -- ← ここで大文字指定
  PATIENT_HASHDIFF:
    columns:
      - "FIRST_NAME"             -- ← 大文字
```

stage マクロの出力カラムは全て大文字になるので、それを参照する Raw Vault（Hub/Link/Sat）も大文字、さらにその上の Business Vault・Information Mart も大文字で統一する必要がありました。

### なぜ automate-dv が大文字を使うか

automate-dv は元々 **Snowflake 向け**に設計されたパッケージです。Snowflake は内部的にカラム名を大文字で管理するため（`SELECT first_name` → 内部では `FIRST_NAME`）、automate-dv のドキュメントやマクロは全て大文字を前提にしています。

Databricks は大文字小文字を区別しない（case-insensitive）ので実害はないですが、automate-dv のマクロが生成する SQL と整合性を保つために大文字に合わせた、という経緯です。

### 小文字にできるか？

Business Vault や Information Mart は手書き SQL なので、小文字に変えることは可能です。ただし Raw Vault 層は automate-dv マクロが生成するカラム名（大文字）をそのまま使うので、層をまたいで命名規則が混在することになります。

name: INSURANCE_HK\n        description: 保険会社ハッシュキー（secondary FK、関係の対象）\n      - name: START_DATE\n        description: 関係の開始日\n      - name: END_DATE\n        description: 関係の終了日（NULL = 現在有効）\n      - name: LOAD_DATETIME\n        description: ロード日時\n      - name: RECORD_SOURCE\n        description: データソース識別子\n```\n\nWrite all 18 files. ### **HUBS (6 files)**

### **LINKS (3 files)**

id: toolu_011ohPVLAtsWFQ4BGC5UJDqM
```
     1→{{ config(materialized='table') }}
     2→
     3→{#
     4→  PIT (Point-In-Time) テーブル: pit_patient
     5→
     6→  目的:
     7→    hub_patient に紐づく複数の Satellite について、
     8→    各時点（as_of_date）での「最新レコード」の LOAD_DATETIME を保持する。
     9→    これにより、任意の時点での患者の状態を効率的にクエリできる。
    10→
    11→  構造:
    12→    PATIENT_HK | AS_OF_DATE | SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS | SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_PK
    13→#}
    14→
    15→WITH as_of_dates AS (
    16→    -- Raw Vault 内の全 LOAD_DATETIME を収集して、PITの時間軸を作る
    17→    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE
    18→    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}
    19→
    20→    UNION
    21→
    22→    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE
    23→    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }}
    24→),
    25→
    26→hub AS (
    27→    SELECT PATIENT_HK
    28→    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}
    29→),
    30→
    31→-- Hub × as_of_date の全組み合わせ
    32→hub_pit AS (
    33→    SELECT
    34→        hub.PATIENT_HK,
    35→        aod.AS_OF_DATE
    36→    FROM hub
    37→    CROSS JOIN as_of_dates aod
    38→),
    39→
    40→-- 各時点での sat_patient_details の最新 LOAD_DATETIME を特定
    41→sat_patient_lookup AS (
    42→    SELECT
    43→        hp.PATIENT_HK,
    44→        hp.AS_OF_DATE,
    45→        MAX(spd.LOAD_DATETIME) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS
    46→    FROM hub_pit hp
    47→    LEFT JOIN {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }} spd
    48→        ON hp.PATIENT_HK = spd.PATIENT_HK
    49→        AND spd.LOAD_DATETIME <= hp.AS_OF_DATE
    50→    GROUP BY hp.PATIENT_HK, hp.AS_OF_DATE
    51→)
    52→
    53→SELECT
    54→    spl.PATIENT_HK,
    55→    spl.AS_OF_DATE,
    56→    COALESCE(spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS, CAST('1900-01-01' AS TIMESTAMP)) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS
    57→FROM sat_patient_lookup spl
    58→WHERE spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS IS NOT NULL
    59→ORDER BY spl.PATIENT_HK, spl.AS_OF_DATE
    60→
```

     1→{{ config(materialized='table') }}
     2→
     3→{#
     4→  Bridge テーブル: bridge_patient_medication
     5→
     6→  目的:
     7→    hub_patient から hub_medication までの多段 JOIN パスを事前結合する。
     8→    通常のクエリでは patient → link_visit → link_prescription → medication と
     9→    4テーブルの JOIN が必要だが、Bridge があれば1テーブルで済む。
    10→
    11→  パス:
    12→    hub_patient → link_visit → hub_visit → link_prescription → hub_medication
    13→#}
    14→
    15→WITH patient_visits AS (
    16→    -- 患者と診察の関係（link_visit から）
    17→    SELECT
    18→        lv.LINK_VISIT_HK,
    19→        lv.PATIENT_HK,
    20→        lv.VISIT_HK
    21→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    22→),
    23→
    24→visit_prescriptions AS (
    25→    -- 診察と処方の関係（link_prescription から）
    26→    SELECT
    27→        lp.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,
    28→        lp.VISIT_HK,
    29→        lp.MEDICATION_HK
    30→    FROM {{ ref('link_prescription') }} lp
    31→)
    32→
    33→SELECT
    34→    pv.PATIENT_HK,
    35→    pv.LINK_VISIT_HK,
    36→    pv.VISIT_HK,
    37→    vp.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,
    38→    vp.MEDICATION_HK
    39→FROM patient_visits pv
    40→INNER JOIN visit_prescriptions vp
    41→    ON pv.VISIT_HK = vp.VISIT_HK
    42→

     9→    通常の Satellite は「ソースからの事実」を記録するが、
    10→    Computed Satellite は「計算・集約された派生データ」を保持する。
    11→
    12→  計算内容:
    13→    - 総診察回数、総治療費、平均治療費
    14→    - 担当医師数（ユニーク）
    15→    - 初回/最終診察日
    16→    - 処方された薬の種類数
    17→#}
    18→
    19→WITH patient_visits AS (
    20→    SELECT
    21→        lv.PATIENT_HK,
    22→        lv.VISIT_HK,
    23→        lv.DOCTOR_HK,
    24→        svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,
    25→        svd.TREATMENT_COST,
    26→        svd.LOAD_DATETIME
    27→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    28→    INNER JOIN {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }} svd
    29→        ON lv.VISIT_HK = svd.VISIT_HK
    30→),
    31→
    32→patient_prescriptions AS (
    33→    SELECT
    34→        lv.PATIENT_HK,
    35→        lp.MEDICATION_HK
    36→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    37→    INNER JOIN {{ ref('link_prescription') }} lp
    38→        ON lv.VISIT_HK = lp.VISIT_HK
    39→),
    40→
    41→visit_summary AS (
    42→    SELECT
    43→        PATIENT_HK,
    44→        COUNT(DISTINCT VISIT_HK) AS TOTAL_VISITS,
    45→        COUNT(DISTINCT DOCTOR_HK) AS DISTINCT_DOCTORS,
    46→        COUNT(DISTINCT DIAGNOSIS_CODE) AS DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,
    47→        SUM(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,
    48→        AVG(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS AVG_TREATMENT_COST,
    49→        MIN(LOAD_DATETIME) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE,
    50→        MAX(LOAD_DATETIME) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE
    51→    FROM patient_visits
    52→    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK
    53→),
    54→
    55→prescription_summary AS (
    56→    SELECT
    57→        PATIENT_HK,
    58→        COUNT(DISTINCT MEDICATION_HK) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS
    59→    FROM patient_prescriptions
    60→    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK
    61→)
    62→
    63→SELECT
    64→    vs.PATIENT_HK,
    65→    vs.TOTAL_VISITS,
    66→    vs.DISTINCT_DOCTORS,
    67→    vs.DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,
    68→    vs.TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,
    69→    vs.AVG_TREATMENT_COST,
    70→    vs.FIRST_VISIT_DATE,
    71→    vs.LAST_VISIT_DATE,
    72→    COALESCE(ps.DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS, 0) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS,
    73→    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS LOAD_DATETIME,
    74→    'COMPUTED' AS RECORD_SOURCE
    75→FROM visit_summary vs
    76→LEFT JOIN prescription_summary ps
    77→    ON vs.PATIENT_HK = ps.PATIENT_HK
    78→
```

> AGENT

実は `dbt_project.yml` で既にディレクトリレベルで設定済みです:

| ディレクトリ | dbt_project.yml | SQLファイル側 |
|---|---|---|
| `raw_vault/` | `+materialized: incremental` | `incremental` (重複) |
| `business_vault/` | `+materialized: table` | `table` (重複) |
| `marts/` | `+materialized: table` | `table` (重複) |

つまり、SQLファイルの `{{ config(materialized='...') }}` はすべて冗長です。削除できます。

唯一の例外は `eff_sat_patient_insurance.sql` で、`meta` 設定があるので config ブロック自体は残しつつ `materialized` だけ除去します。

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01PnjMY9QgN3A1HeVgSWVPCx
```json
{
  "command": "# Remove {{ config(materialized='table') }} or {{ config(materialized='incremental') }} lines\n# from all datavault SQL files (not eff_sat or time_spine)\nfor f in \\\n  transform/fusion/models/raw_vault/hubs/*.sql \\\n  transform/fusion/models/raw_vault/links/*.sql \\\n  transform/fusion/models/raw_vault/satellites/*.sql \\\n  transform/fusion/models/business_vault/*.sql \\\n  transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/*.sql; do\n  # Skip eff_sat which has extra meta config\n  if echo \"$f\" | grep -q \"eff_sat\"; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    # Remove the config line and the blank line after it\n    sed -i '' '/^{{ config(materialized=/d' \"$f\" && \\\n    sed -i '' '1{/^$/d;}' \"$f\"\n    echo \"Cleaned: $f\"\n  fi\ndone",
  "description": "Remove redundant materialized config from SQL files"
}
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
dbtの `materialized` 設定には優先順位があります: **SQLファイルの config() > dbt_project.yml**。同じ値なら `dbt_project.yml` に集約するのがDRY原則に沿っています。個別のSQLファイルで config() を使うのは、ディレクトリのデフォルトと**異なる**設定が必要な場合だけにするのがベストプラクティスです。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

27ファイルから冗長な `materialized` 設定を除去しました。`dbt_project.yml` の一箇所で管理されるようになっています。

> AGENT

**はい、冪等です。** コンパイルされたSQLに仕組みが見えます。

---

## 各エンティティ別の冪等性の仕組み

### Hub / Link — `LEFT ANTI JOIN` で重複排除

```sql
-- hub_patient (compiled)
records_to_insert AS (
    SELECT ...
    FROM row_rank_1 AS a
    LEFT ANTI JOIN hub_patient AS d   -- ← 既存テーブルと照合
    ON a.PATIENT_HK = d.PATIENT_HK   -- ← HKが既にあれば除外
)
```

ハッシュキーが既に存在するレコードは `LEFT ANTI JOIN` で弾かれるので、同じデータを何回 `dbt run` しても **新しい行は挿入されません**。

### Satellite — `HASHDIFF` + `LAG` で変更検知

```sql
-- sat_patient_details (compiled)
QUALIFY sd.PATIENT_HASHDIFF !=
    LAG(sd.PATIENT_HASHDIFF, 1,
        COALESCE(lr.HASHDIFF, 'FFFFFFFF')  -- ← 既存の最新HASHDIFFと比較
    ) OVER (
        PARTITION BY sd.PATIENT_HK
        ORDER BY sd.LOAD_DATETIME ASC
    )
```

既存テーブルの最新 `HASHDIFF` とソースの `HASHDIFF` を比較し、**値が変わっていなければ挿入しない**。同じデータで再実行しても重複行は生まれません。

### まとめ

| エンティティ | 冪等性の仕組み | 判定キー |
|---|---|---|
| **Hub** | `LEFT ANTI JOIN` on HK | ハッシュキー (PK) |
| **Link** | `LEFT ANTI JOIN` on HK | ハッシュキー (PK) |
| **Satellite** | `HASHDIFF` 比較 + `LAG` | ハッシュキー + HASHDIFF |

これはData Vault 2.0の「insert-only」原則と整合しています。UPDATE/DELETEは行わず、変更があったときだけ新しい行をINSERTするが、同じ状態の再ロードでは何も起きない、という設計です。

}

> DEVELOPER

Use revamped materializations based on separating create and insert.  This allows more performant column comments, as well as new column features.
You may opt into the new behavior sooner by setting `flags.use_materialization_v2` to `True` in `dbt_project.yml`.

The PR includes 8 seed CSV files with medical data (patients, doctors, visits, prescriptions, medications, insurance), 35 dbt models across all vault layers, comprehensive YAML schema documentation for all models, and detailed learning notes documenting Data Vault concepts and implementation patterns.\n\n**Changes:**\n- Added automate-dv package (v0.11.5) for Data Vault 2.0 modeling patterns\n- tool_result
id: toolu_01PoTY5EvhTZosTdvGetSypf
```
     1→WITH as_of_dates AS (
     2→    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE
     3→    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}
     4→
     5→    UNION
     6→
     7→    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE
     8→    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }}
     9→),
    10→
    11→hub AS (
    12→    SELECT PATIENT_HK
    13→    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}
    14→),
    15→
    16→hub_pit AS (
    17→    SELECT
    18→        hub.PATIENT_HK,
    19→        aod.AS_OF_DATE
    20→    FROM hub
    21→    CROSS JOIN as_of_dates aod
    22→),
    23→
    24→sat_patient_lookup AS (
    25→    SELECT
    26→        hp.PATIENT_HK,
    27→        hp.AS_OF_DATE,
    28→        MAX(spd.LOAD_DATETIME) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS
    29→    FROM hub_pit hp
    30→    LEFT JOIN {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }} spd
    31→        ON hp.PATIENT_HK = spd.PATIENT_HK
    32→        AND spd.LOAD_DATETIME <= hp.AS_OF_DATE
    33→    GROUP BY hp.PATIENT_HK, hp.AS_OF_DATE
    34→)
    35→
    36→SELECT
    37→    spl.PATIENT_HK,
    38→    spl.AS_OF_DATE,
    39→    COALESCE(spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS, CAST('1900-01-01' AS TIMESTAMP)) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS
    40→FROM sat_patient_lookup spl
    41→WHERE spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS IS NOT NULL
    42→ORDER BY spl.PATIENT_HK, spl.AS_OF_DATE
    43→
```

     6→        svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,
     7→        svd.TREATMENT_COST,
     8→        svd.LOAD_DATETIME
     9→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    10→    INNER JOIN {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }} svd
    11→        ON lv.VISIT_HK = svd.VISIT_HK
    12→),
    13→
    14→patient_prescriptions AS (
    15→    SELECT
    16→        lv.PATIENT_HK,
    17→        lp.MEDICATION_HK
    18→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    19→    INNER JOIN {{ ref('link_prescription') }} lp
    20→        ON lv.VISIT_HK = lp.VISIT_HK
    21→),
    22→
    23→visit_summary AS (
    24→    SELECT
    25→        PATIENT_HK,
    26→        COUNT(DISTINCT VISIT_HK) AS TOTAL_VISITS,
    27→        COUNT(DISTINCT DOCTOR_HK) AS DISTINCT_DOCTORS,
    28→        COUNT(DISTINCT DIAGNOSIS_CODE) AS DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,
    29→        SUM(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,
    30→        AVG(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS AVG_TREATMENT_COST,
    31→        MIN(LOAD_DATETIME) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE,
    32→        MAX(LOAD_DATETIME) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE
    33→    FROM patient_visits
    34→    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK
    35→),
    36→
    37→prescription_summary AS (
    38→    SELECT
    39→        PATIENT_HK,
    40→        COUNT(DISTINCT MEDICATION_HK) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS
    41→    FROM patient_prescriptions
    42→    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK
    43→)
    44→
    45→SELECT
    46→    vs.PATIENT_HK,
    47→    vs.TOTAL_VISITS,
    48→    vs.DISTINCT_DOCTORS,
    49→    vs.DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,
    50→    vs.TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,
    51→    vs.AVG_TREATMENT_COST,
    52→    vs.FIRST_VISIT_DATE,
    53→    vs.LAST_VISIT_DATE,
    54→    COALESCE(ps.DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS, 0) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS,
    55→    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS LOAD_DATETIME,
    56→    'COMPUTED' AS RECORD_SOURCE
    57→FROM visit_summary vs
    58→LEFT JOIN prescription_summary ps
    59→    ON vs.PATIENT_HK = ps.PATIENT_HK
    60→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/business_vault/sat_patient_visit_summary.sql",
  "old_string": "WITH patient_visits AS (\n    SELECT\n        lv.PATIENT_HK,\n        lv.VISIT_HK,\n        lv.DOCTOR_HK,\n        svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n        svd.TREATMENT_COST,\n        svd.LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\n    INNER JOIN {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }} svd\n        ON lv.VISIT_HK = svd.VISIT_HK\n),",
  "new_string": "WITH visit_details_latest AS (\n    SELECT\n        VISIT_HK,\n        DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n        TREATMENT_COST,\n        LOAD_DATETIME,\n        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n            PARTITION BY VISIT_HK\n            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n        ) AS rn\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n),\n\npatient_visits AS (\n    SELECT\n        lv.PATIENT_HK,\n        lv.VISIT_HK,\n        lv.DOCTOR_HK,\n        svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n        svd.TREATMENT_COST,\n        svd.LOAD_DATETIME\n    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\n    INNER JOIN visit_details_latest svd\n        ON lv.VISIT_HK = svd.VISIT_HK\n        AND svd.rn = 1\n),"
}
```

  "content": "-- link_visit の FK がすべて対応する Hub に存在することを検証する。\n-- 行が返ればテスト失敗（孤立した FK がある）。\nSELECT lv.LINK_VISIT_HK, 'PATIENT_HK' AS missing_in\nFROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_patient') }} hp ON lv.PATIENT_HK = hp.PATIENT_HK\nWHERE hp.PATIENT_HK IS NULL\n\nUNION ALL\n\nSELECT lv.LINK_VISIT_HK, 'DOCTOR_HK' AS missing_in\nFROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_doctor') }} hd ON lv.DOCTOR_HK = hd.DOCTOR_HK\nWHERE hd.DOCTOR_HK IS NULL\n\nUNION ALL\n\nSELECT lv.LINK_VISIT_HK, 'VISIT_HK' AS missing_in\nFROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_visit') }} hv ON lv.VISIT_HK = hv.VISIT_HK\nWHERE hv.VISIT_HK IS NULL\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/tests/datavault/satellite_no_orphan_records.sql",
  "content": "-- Satellite レコードが対応する Hub/Link を持つことを検証する。\n-- 孤立した Satellite レコードがあればテスト失敗。\nSELECT spd.PATIENT_HK, 'sat_patient_details' AS satellite_name\nFROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }} spd\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_patient') }} hp ON spd.PATIENT_HK = hp.PATIENT_HK\nWHERE hp.PATIENT_HK IS NULL\n\nUNION ALL\n\nSELECT sdd.DOCTOR_HK, 'sat_doctor_details' AS satellite_name\nFROM {{ ref('sat_doctor_details') }} sdd\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_doctor') }} hd ON sdd.DOCTOR_HK = hd.DOCTOR_HK\nWHERE hd.DOCTOR_HK IS NULL\n\nUNION ALL\n\nSELECT svd.VISIT_HK, 'sat_visit_details' AS satellite_name\nFROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }} svd\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_visit') }} hv ON svd.VISIT_HK = hv.VISIT_HK\nWHERE hv.VISIT_HK IS NULL\n\nUNION ALL\n\nSELECT smd.MEDICATION_HK, 'sat_medication_details' AS satellite_name\nFROM {{ ref('sat_medication_details') }} smd\nLEFT JOIN {{ ref('hub_medication') }} hm ON smd.MEDICATION_HK = hm.MEDICATION_HK\nWHERE hm.MEDICATION_HK IS NULL\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/tests/datavault/fct_prescriptions_cost_calculation.sql",
  "content": "-- fct_prescriptions の ESTIMATED_COST が DURATION_DAYS * UNIT_PRICE と一致することを検証する。\n-- UNIT_PRICE が NULL の場合は ESTIMATED_COST も NULL であるべき。\nSELECT\n    LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK,\n    DURATION_DAYS,\n    UNIT_PRICE,\n    ESTIMATED_COST,\n    DURATION_DAYS * UNIT_PRICE AS expected_cost\nFROM {{ ref('fct_prescriptions') }}\nWHERE UNIT_PRICE IS NOT NULL\n  AND ESTIMATED_COST != DURATION_DAYS * UNIT_PRICE\n"
}

`tests-path` は dbt のデフォルトが `tests/` なので `dbt_project.yml` への追加は不要です。

I've found comprehensive information about the Puppini Bridge and its relationship to data vault modeling. Here are the key findings:

## Overview

A Puppini bridge refers to a table in a database that is structured in a particular way - its purpose being to map join keys to one another. This is one part of the "Unified Star Schema", a structure developed by Francesco Puppini.

## Data Vault Connection

Thanks to the data vault approach, all the necessary information is available to find correlated entities. This manual operation was later automated. The Puppini Bridge table eliminates some of the most common problems in dimensional modeling and enables a more effortless self-service BI experience.

## Technical Structure

The Puppini Bridge table is a matrix-like table where columns list primary keys of each data warehouse table (fact or dimension) plus one extra column called Stage. The number of columns equals the number of tables in the data warehouse (dimensions and facts) plus one. Rows are a result of union all operation of all data warehouse tables carrying only the key columns. The number of rows in a Bridge table is a sum of all records in the data warehouse (fact and dimensions).

## Problem Resolution

The Unified Star Schema natively prevents fan and chasm traps from happening. No data can ever be duplicated because it uses union instead of join.

# The Puppini Bridge: A Solution to Complex Data Modeling

## What It Is

The Puppini Bridge is a central mapping table that represents part of the "Unified Star Schema," a data modeling approach developed by Francesco Puppini. Rather than traditional star schemas where fact tables join directly to dimensions, this structure places a bridge table at the center, with all other tables joining outward to it.

## Core Problem It Solves

Data modeling faces two critical challenges:

**Fan-outs**: When joining tables with one-to-many relationships, rows duplicate, inflating aggregate results. For example, if an orders table joins a products table where products have multiple price history rows, summing order values produces incorrect revenue figures.

**Chasm traps**: These occur when multiple fact tables connect indirectly through dimensions, creating unintended many-to-many relationships. A typical scenario involves orders → products → shipments, where both fact tables' measures become artificially inflated.

## How It Works

The bridge table contains composite keys mapping to all relevant entities. As described in the article, the bridge includes multiple "stages" (like Line Items, Users, Products), each creating rows with mapped keys using UNION ALL logic. Every join from the bridge outward maintains a many-to-one relationship.

**Key principle**: "As long as you start your query with the bridge table and define aggregations against the bridge table itself, you can join without worrying about chained joins."

## Implementation Structure

A bridge table typically includes:
- A stage identifier (distinguishing data origin)
- Multiple key columns (one per dimension)
- Aggregated base metrics defined at the bridge level

All downstream tables join to the bridge using these keys, ensuring relationships remain explicit and controllable.

## Advantages Over Traditional Approaches

Unlike standard star schemas vulnerable to chasm traps, the Puppini Bridge eliminates complex join chains by design. Semantic layers normally struggle with indirect relationship problems, but this approach makes them manageable through deliberate architecture.
```

## Puppini Bridge（Unified Star Schema）

**Puppini Bridge** は、Francesco Puppini が考案した **Unified Star Schema (USS)** の中核となるテーブルです。従来のスター・スキーマのファン・トラップやキャズム・トラップを構造的に防ぐためのアプローチです。

---

### 従来のスター・スキーマの問題

**Fan Trap（ファン・トラップ）**
```
orders ──→ products ──→ price_history（1:N）
```
products に対して price_history が複数行あると、JOIN で行が膨張し、`SUM(order_amount)` が不正に膨らむ。

**Chasm Trap（キャズム・トラップ）**
```
orders ──→ products ←── shipments
```
2つのファクトが間接的に多対多で接続され、両方のメジャーが膨張する。

---

### Puppini Bridge の構造

通常のスター・スキーマではファクトとディメンションを直接 JOIN するが、Puppini Bridge では**すべてのテーブルのキーを1つの Bridge テーブルに集約**する。

```
            dim_customer
                ↑
fct_orders → BRIDGE ← dim_product
                ↓
           fct_shipments
```

Bridge テーブルの構造:

| STAGE | customer_key | product_key | order_key | shipment_key |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| orders | C-001 | P-001 | O-001 | NULL |
| orders | C-001 | P-002 | O-002 | NULL |
| shipments | NULL | P-001 | NULL | S-001 |
| shipments | NULL | P-002 | NULL | S-002 |
| customers | C-001 | NULL | NULL | NULL |

**特徴:**
- カラム数 = DWH内のテーブル数（Fact + Dim）+ 1（STAGE列）
- 行数 = 全テーブルの行数の合計（`UNION ALL` で構築）
- Bridge からの JOIN はすべて **多対一** になるため、行の膨張が起きない

---

### 今作った Data Vault の Bridge との違い

| | Data Vault Bridge | Puppini Bridge |
|---|---|---|
| **目的** | 多ホップ JOIN の事前計算 | Fan/Chasm Trap の防止 |
| **スコープ** | 特定のパス（Patient → Medication） | DWH全体のキーマッピング |
| **構造** | HK のリスト | 全テーブルの PK + STAGE列 |
| **結合方向** | Bridge → Hub/Link/Sat | Bridge → 全 Fact/Dim |
| **用途** | クエリパフォーマンス最適化 | セルフサービスBI、セマンティックレイヤー |

Data Vault の Bridge は「クエリ高速化」のための事前JOIN、Puppini Bridge は「結合の安全性」のためのアーキテクチャパターンという位置づけです。

---

Sources:
- [The Bridge of Puppini](https://davidsj.substack.com/p/the-bridge-of-puppini)
- [The Unified Star Schema Paradigm Review | Towards Data Science](https://towardsdatascience.com/the-new-unified-star-schema-paradigm-in-analytics-data-modeling-review-a245b2641dc8/)
- [Bill Inmon and Francesco Puppini: Building the Unified Star Schema | DVUG](https://www.ukdatavaultusergroup.co.uk/bill-inmon-francesco-puppini-building-the-unified-star-schema/)
- [Exploring the Unified Star Schema with Francesco Puppini](https://www.revolt.bi/en/exploring-the-unified-star-schema-with-francesco-puppini/)

```json
{
  "id": "TA-374",
  "description": "## 概要\n\nPuppini Bridge（Unified Star Schema）を dbt Fusion 上で実装し、Fan Trap / Chasm Trap を構造的に防ぐデータモデリングパターンを学ぶ。\n\n## 背景\n\n- Francesco Puppini と Bill Inmon が提唱した Unified Star Schema (USS)\n- 従来のスター・スキーマで発生する Fan Trap / Chasm Trap を Bridge テーブルで構造的に解決\n- Data Vault の Bridge（多ホップJOIN最適化）とは異なるアプローチ\n\n## Puppini Bridge の特徴\n\n- DWH 内の全テーブル（Fact + Dim）のキーを1つの Bridge テーブルに集約\n- カラム数 = テーブル数 + 1（STAGE 列）\n- 行数 = 全テーブルの行数合計（UNION ALL で構築）\n- Bridge からの JOIN はすべて多対一 → 行膨張が起きない\n\n## ドメイン: 旅行予約\n\nChasm Trap が自然に発生するドメインとして旅行予約を採用。\n\n### エンティティ設計\n\n```mermaid\nerDiagram\n    TRAVELER ||--o{ FLIGHT_BOOKING : books\n    TRAVELER ||--o{ HOTEL_BOOKING : reserves\n    FLIGHT_BOOKING }o--|| DESTINATION : flies_to\n    HOTEL_BOOKING }o--|| DESTINATION : stays_at\n    FLIGHT_BOOKING }o--|| AIRLINE : operated_by\n    HOTEL_BOOKING }o--|| HOTEL : stays_at\n    HOTEL }o--|| DESTINATION : located_in\n```\n\n### Trap の発生パターン\n\n**Chasm Trap**: フライト予約とホテル予約が旅行者/目的地ディメンションを共有\n- `fct_flight_bookings → dim_traveler ← fct_hotel_bookings`\n- 従来のスター・スキーマでJOINすると両方のメジャーが膨張\n\n**Fan Trap**: ホテルの料金履歴（シーズン別料金変動）\n- `fct_hotel_bookings → dim_hotel → hotel_rate_history (1:N)`\n- 料金変動テーブルをJOINすると予約金額が重複カウント\n\n### テーブル構成\n\n**Seeds（ダミーデータ）**:\n- `raw_travelers`: 旅行者（20件）\n- `raw_destinations`: 目的地（10件）\n- `raw_airlines`: 航空会社（5件）\n- `raw_hotels`: ホテル（15件）\n- `raw_flight_bookings`: フライト予約（30件）\n- `raw_hotel_bookings`: ホテル予約（25件）\n- `raw_hotel_rate_history`: ホテル料金履歴（45件、Fan Trap 再現用）\n\n**Models**:\n- Staging: 7 models\n- Dimensions: `dim_traveler`, `dim_destination`, `dim_airline`, `dim_hotel`\n- Facts: `fct_flight_bookings`, `fct_hotel_bookings`\n- Puppini Bridge: `bridge_unified` (UNION ALL of all tables)\n\n## 実装計画\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    S[Seeds / ダミーデータ] --> STG[Staging]\n    STG --> DIM[Dimensions]\n    STG --> FCT[Facts]\n    DIM --> BR[Puppini Bridge]\n    FCT --> BR\n    BR --> TEST[Singular Tests]\n    TEST --> CMP[従来スキーマとの比較]\n```\n\n1. ダミーデータ（Seeds）を作成\n2. Staging → Dimension / Fact を構築\n3. Puppini Bridge テーブルを UNION ALL で構築\n4. Bridge 経由のクエリで Fan/Chasm Trap が発生しないことを singular テストで検証\n5. 従来のスター・スキーマとの比較（同じデータで両方作り、集計結果の違いを確認）\n\n## 参考\n\n- [The Bridge of Puppini](https://davidsj.substack.com/p/the-bridge-of-puppini)\n- [Unified Star Schema Paradigm Review](https://towardsdatascience.com/the-new-unified-star-schema-paradigm-in-analytics-data-modeling-review-a245b2641dc8/)\n- [Bill Inmon &amp; Francesco Puppini: Building the USS](https://www.ukdatavaultusergroup.co.uk/bill-inmon-francesco-puppini-building-the-unified-star-schema/)\n"
}
```

> AGENT

**[TA-374](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-374/puppini-bridge-unified-star-schema-の学習実装)** を旅行予約ドメインで更新しました。

旅行予約は Puppini Bridge の学習に最適です:

- **Chasm Trap**: `fct_flight_bookings → dim_traveler ← fct_hotel_bookings`（2つの Fact が Dim を共有）
- **Fan Trap**: `fct_hotel_bookings → dim_hotel → hotel_rate_history`（ホテルにシーズン別料金が複数行）

テーブル構成:
- Seeds 7つ（travelers, destinations, airlines, hotels, flight_bookings, hotel_bookings, hotel_rate_history）
- Dim 4つ + Fact 2つ + Puppini Bridge 1つ
- 従来スター・スキーマとの比較テストも含む

取り組む際にはお声がけください。

経過順調,5500,2026-01-26,HOSPITAL_SYSTEM\n```","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:16Z","line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/seeds/datavault/raw_visits.csv"},{"author":"coderabbitai[bot]","body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🧩 Analysis chain\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`dbt-databricks use_materialization_v2 full-refresh incremental DROP TABLE bug`\n\n💡 Result:\n\nWhat you’re seeing is a known behavior/bug pattern in the Spark-derived incremental materialization: on `dbt run --full-refresh` for an **incremental** model, the materialization can **explicitly drop the existing relation** (DROP TABLE via `adapter.drop_relation`) before rebuilding, even when the underlying engine could have used **`CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE`** (notably for Delta). This unnecessarily removes table-level grants/ACLs and can have other side effects. [1]\n\nIn **dbt-databricks**, the newer materialization work gated by `flags: { use_materialization_v2: true }` was introduced in the 1.10 line [2], and later release notes indicate changes specifically aimed at avoiding “DROP + CREATE” where `CREATE OR REPLACE` is appropriate and enabling “create or replace semantics on full refresh in Mat V2”. [3] Practically, the remediation path is:\n\n- **Upgrade dbt-databricks to a version that includes the Mat V2 “create/replace on full-refresh” work**, and enable `use_materialization_v2`. [2][3]  \n- If you can’t upgrade, the common workaround is a **custom incremental materialization** that skips `drop_relation` for Delta and relies on create/replace semantics (the approach suggested in the original bug report). [1]\n\n**Sources:**  \n[1] dbt-spark CT-220 issue mirror describing the `drop_relation`-before-rebuild behavior and its consequences (grants removed) — https://lightrun.com/answers/dbt-labs-dbt-spark-ct-220-dont-drop-table-first-for-incremental-full-refresh-if-using-delta-file-format  \n[2] dbt-databricks v1.10.0 release summary noting introduction of `use_materialization_v2` — https://newreleases.io/project/github/databricks/dbt-databricks/release/v1.10.0  \n[3] dbt-databricks v1.11.0 release summary noting “Materialized views now uses CREATE OR REPLACE … instead of DROP + CREATE” and “Allow create or replace semantics on full refresh in Mat V2” — https://newreleases.io/project/github/databricks/dbt-databricks/release/v1.11.0\n\n---\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`dbt-databricks use_materialization_v2 view schema default resolved`\n\n💡 Result:\n\n`use_materialization_v2` in **dbt-databricks** changes *how* views are updated (for example, `view_update_via_alter` / safer swaps), but it **does not change how the target schema is chosen**; schema (and catalog) resolution is still standard dbt naming logic. [3]\n\n### How the **schema** is resolved for a `view` when `schema` is “default”\nIn dbt, “default” effectively means **no custom schema was supplied**, so dbt uses your **target schema** (`target.schema`) from the profile. [1]\n\nIf you *do* set `schema: \u003ccustom\u003e`, dbt’s default behavior is to build in:\n`\u003ctarget_schema\u003e_\u003ccustom_schema\u003e` (via the `generate_schema_name` macro). [1]\n\n### How that maps on **Databricks / Unity Catalog**\nWith `dbt-databricks`, your profile defines:\n- `catalog:` (optional) → the Unity Catalog **catalog** (dbt often calls this the “database” conceptually)\n- `schema:` → the Unity Catalog **schema** where models (including `view`s) are created by default [2]\n\nSo a view ends up at:\n- `\u003ccatalog\u003e.\u003ctarget_schema\u003e.\u003cview_name\u003e` if `schema` is default/none\n- `\u003ccatalog\u003e.\u003ctarget_schema\u003e_\u003ccustom_schema\u003e.\u003cview_name\u003e` if `schema: \u003ccustom_schema\u003e` is set (unless you override `generate_schema_name`). [1][2]\n\n---\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`dbt-databricks use_materialization_v2 experimental status 2025 2026`\n\n💡 Result:\n\n`use_materialization_v2` in **dbt-databricks** is an **opt-in behavior/feature flag** that gates the “Materialization V2” revamps (tables/views/seeds, safer replace patterns, etc.). It was introduced in the **1.10.0** line (alpha pre-release first, then 1.10.0 GA) and, as of the latest 2026-era releases, it is **still controlled by the flag (i.e., not the default behavior)**—so functionally it remains **“experimental/rollout-gated” rather than fully default-on**. [1][2][3]\n\n**Timeline (key points)**\n- **Nov 19, 2024**: PR merged introducing the V2 materialization gating flag. [4]\n- **dbt-databricks 1.10.0 (Apr 8, 2025)**: changelog explicitly notes “Introduced `use_materialization_v2` flag for gating materialization revamps.” [2]\n- **Jan 12, 2026**: PyPI shows latest release **1.11.4**, indicating the adapter is well past initial rollout but still uses flags for behavior gating (including this one). [3]\n\n## Sources\n[1] dbt-databricks v1.10.0a1 release notes (NewReleases) — “Introduced use_materialization_v2 flag…” https://newreleases.io/project/github/databricks/dbt-databricks/release/v1.10.0a1  \n[2] dbt-databricks CHANGELOG.md (raw) — 1.10.0 entry introducing the flag https://raw.githubusercontent.com/databricks/dbt-databricks/main/CHANGELOG.md  \n[3] dbt-databricks on PyPI — release history showing 1.11.4 on Jan 12, 2026 https://pypi.org/project/dbt-databricks/  \n[4] GitHub PR `#844` — “V2 Materialization flag introduced” (merged Nov 19, 2024) https://github.com/databricks/dbt-databricks/pull/844\n\n---\n\n\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n**Review use_materialization_v2 flag status; original concerns have been addressed in recent dbt-databricks releases.**\n\nThe `--full-refresh` issue for incremental models has been resolved since dbt-databricks 1.11.0 (released with \"create or replace semantics on full refresh in Mat V2\"), which specifically fixes the `DROP TABLE` problem by using `CREATE OR REPLACE` instead. With current versions (1.11.4+), enabling `use_materialization_v2` is actually the recommended approach to avoid the original full-refresh issue.\n\nThe schema claim requires clarification: `use_materialization_v2` does not change how target schemas are resolved. View schema assignment still follows standard dbt naming logic via the `generate_schema_name` macro, regardless of whether Mat V2 is enabled.\n\nThe flag remains opt-in by design (not enabled by default) as a deliberate rollout gate, not due to active unresolved bugs. If targeting a recent dbt-databricks version, consider enabling this flag to benefit from the full-refresh fix and other materialization improvements.\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🤖 Prompt for AI Agents\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@transform/fusion/dbt_project.yml` around lines 6 - 8, The review says the\nuse_materialization_v2 flag should be enabled for modern dbt-databricks versions\nbecause the full-refresh DROP TABLE issue was fixed in 1.11.0+, so update the\nflags block by setting use_materialization_v2: true (or ensure it remains true)\nin the dbt_project.yml flags section and remove or update any accompanying\ncomment that incorrectly claims Mat V2 changes schema resolution; explicitly\nstate that schema resolution still uses generate_schema_name and that Mat V2 is\nopt-in by design for rollout gating.\n```\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro --\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit --\u003e","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:46:45Z","line":8,"path":"transform/fusion/dbt_project.yml"},{"author":"coderabbitai[bot]","body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n**`FIRST_VISIT_DATE`/`LAST_VISIT_DATE` reflect ETL load time, not clinical visit dates.**\n\n`LOAD_DATETIME` from `sat_visit_details` is a technical vault metadata field recording when the row was ingested — it is not a business-meaningful visit date.     71→    ON pl.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK = b.LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK
    72→LEFT JOIN medication_sat ms
    73→    ON pl.MEDICATION_HK = ms.MEDICATION_HK
    74→    AND ms.rn = 1
    75→LEFT JOIN visit_sat vs
    76→    ON pl.VISIT_HK = vs.VISIT_HK
    77→    AND vs.rn = 1
    78→
```

```
     1→WITH as_of_dates AS (
     2→    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE
     3→    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}
     4→
     5→    UNION
     6→
     7→    SELECT DISTINCT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE
     8→    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }}
     9→),
    10→
    11→hub AS (
    12→    SELECT PATIENT_HK
    13→    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}
    14→),
    15→
    16→hub_pit AS (
    17→    SELECT
    18→        hub.PATIENT_HK,
    19→        aod.AS_OF_DATE
    20→    FROM hub
    21→    CROSS JOIN as_of_dates aod
    22→),
    23→
    24→sat_patient_lookup AS (
    25→    SELECT
    26→        hp.PATIENT_HK,
    27→        hp.AS_OF_DATE,
    28→        MAX(spd.LOAD_DATETIME) AS SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS
    29→    FROM hub_pit hp
    30→    LEFT JOIN {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }} spd
    31→        ON hp.PATIENT_HK = spd.PATIENT_HK
    32→        AND spd.LOAD_DATETIME <= hp.AS_OF_DATE
    33→    GROUP BY hp.PATIENT_HK, hp.AS_OF_DATE
    34→)
    35→
    36→SELECT
    37→    spl.PATIENT_HK,
    38→    spl.AS_OF_DATE,
    39→    spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS
    40→FROM sat_patient_lookup spl
    41→WHERE spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS IS NOT NULL
    42→ORDER BY spl.PATIENT_HK, spl.AS_OF_DATE
    43→
```

```
     1→WITH visit_details_latest AS (
     2→    SELECT
     3→        VISIT_HK,
     4→        DIAGNOSIS_CODE,
     5→        TREATMENT_COST,
     6→        LOAD_DATETIME,
     7→        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
     8→            PARTITION BY VISIT_HK
     9→            ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC
    10→        ) AS rn
    11→    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}
    12→),
    13→
    14→patient_visits AS (
    15→    SELECT
    16→        lv.PATIENT_HK,
    17→        lv.VISIT_HK,
    18→        lv.DOCTOR_HK,
    19→        svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,
    20→        svd.TREATMENT_COST,
    21→        svd.LOAD_DATETIME
    22→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    23→    INNER JOIN visit_details_latest svd
    24→        ON lv.VISIT_HK = svd.VISIT_HK
    25→        AND svd.rn = 1
    26→),
    27→
    28→patient_prescriptions AS (
    29→    SELECT
    30→        lv.PATIENT_HK,
    31→        lp.MEDICATION_HK
    32→    FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv
    33→    INNER JOIN {{ ref('link_prescription') }} lp
    34→        ON lv.VISIT_HK = lp.VISIT_HK
    35→),
    36→
    37→visit_summary AS (
    38→    SELECT
    39→        PATIENT_HK,
    40→        COUNT(DISTINCT VISIT_HK) AS TOTAL_VISITS,
    41→        COUNT(DISTINCT DOCTOR_HK) AS DISTINCT_DOCTORS,
    42→        COUNT(DISTINCT DIAGNOSIS_CODE) AS DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,
    43→        SUM(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,
    44→        AVG(CAST(TREATMENT_COST AS DECIMAL(10, 2))) AS AVG_TREATMENT_COST,
    45→        MIN(LOAD_DATETIME) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE,
    46→        MAX(LOAD_DATETIME) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE
    47→    FROM patient_visits
    48→    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK
    49→),
    50→
    51→prescription_summary AS (
    52→    SELECT
    53→        PATIENT_HK,
    54→        COUNT(DISTINCT MEDICATION_HK) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS
    55→    FROM patient_prescriptions
    56→    GROUP BY PATIENT_HK
    57→)
    58→
    59→SELECT
    60→    vs.PATIENT_HK,
    61→    vs.TOTAL_VISITS,
    62→    vs.DISTINCT_DOCTORS,
    63→    vs.DISTINCT_DIAGNOSES,
    64→    vs.TOTAL_TREATMENT_COST,
    65→    vs.AVG_TREATMENT_COST,
    66→    vs.FIRST_VISIT_DATE,
    67→    vs.LAST_VISIT_DATE,
    68→    COALESCE(ps.DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS, 0) AS DISTINCT_MEDICATIONS,
    69→    CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS LOAD_DATETIME,
    70→    'COMPUTED' AS RECORD_SOURCE
    71→FROM visit_summary vs
    72→LEFT JOIN prescription_summary ps
    73→    ON vs.PATIENT_HK = ps.PATIENT_HK
    74→
```

}

```
     1→WITH date_range AS (
     2→    SELECT
     3→        MIN(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS min_date,
     4→        MAX(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS max_date
     5→    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}
     6→),
     7→
     8→date_series AS (
     9→    SELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL 1 DAY)) AS date_day
    10→    FROM date_range
    11→)
    12→
    13→SELECT
    14→    CAST(date_day AS DATE) AS DATE_KEY,
    15→    YEAR(date_day) AS YEAR,
    16→    MONTH(date_day) AS MONTH,
    17→    DAY(date_day) AS DAY_OF_MONTH,
    18→    DAYOFWEEK(date_day) AS DAY_OF_WEEK,
    19→    CASE DAYOFWEEK(date_day)
    20→        WHEN 1 THEN '日'
    21→        WHEN 2 THEN '月'
    22→        WHEN 3 THEN '火'
    23→        WHEN 4 THEN '水'
    24→        WHEN 5 THEN '木'
    25→        WHEN 6 THEN '金'
    26→        WHEN 7 THEN '土'
    27→    END AS DAY_NAME_JP,
    28→    WEEKOFYEAR(date_day) AS WEEK_OF_YEAR,
    29→    QUARTER(date_day) AS QUARTER,
    30→    CASE
    31→        WHEN DAYOFWEEK(date_day) IN (1, 7) THEN FALSE
    32→        ELSE TRUE
    33→    END AS IS_WEEKDAY,
    34→    DATE_FORMAT(date_day, 'yyyy-MM') AS YEAR_MONTH
    35→FROM date_series
    36→
```
