     1→version: 2
     2→
     3→models:
     4→  - name: eff_sat_patient_insurance
     5→    description: >
     6→      患者保険関係の有効期間を管理する Effectivity Satellite。auto end-dating 有効。
     7→      通常の Satellite が「属性の変更」を検出するのに対し、
     8→      Effectivity Satellite は「関係がいつ有効で、いつ終了したか」を管理する。
     9→    columns:
    10→      - name: LINK_PATIENT_INSURANCE_HK
    11→        description: 患者保険Link ハッシュキー（FK → link_patient_insurance）
    12→        data_tests: [not_null]
    13→      - name: PATIENT_HK
    14→        description: 患者ハッシュキー（driving FK、関係の主体）
    15→      - name: INSURANCE_HK
    16→        description: 保険会社ハッシュキー（secondary FK、関係の対象）
    17→      - name: START_DATE
    18→        description: 関係の開始日
    19→      - name: END_DATE
    20→        description: 関係の終了日（NULL = 現在有効）
    21→      - name: LOAD_DATETIME
    22→        description: ロード日時
    23→      - name: RECORD_SOURCE

   107→
   108→## Seedデータ設計
   109→
   110→### raw_patients (20-30行)
   111→
   112→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   113→|--------|---|------|
   114→| patient_id | string | PK: PAT-001 |
   115→| first_name | string | 名 |
   116→| last_name | string | 姓 |
   117→| date_of_birth | date | 生年月日 |
   118→| gender | string | M/F |
   119→| address | string | 住所 |
   120→| phone | string | 電話番号 |
   121→
   122→### raw_doctors (10-15行)
   123→
   124→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   125→|--------|---|------|
   126→| doctor_id | string | PK: DOC-001 |
   127→| first_name | string | 名 |
   128→| last_name | string | 姓 |
   129→| specialty | string | 専門科 (内科/外科/小児科等) |
   130→| license_number | string | 医師免許番号 |
   131→
   132→### raw_departments (8-10行)
   133→
   134→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   135→|--------|---|------|
   136→| department_id | string | PK: DEPT-001 |
   137→| department_name | string | 部門名 |
   138→| floor | integer | 階数 |
   139→| phone_extension | string | 内線番号 |
   140→
   141→### raw_medications (15-20行)
   142→
   143→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   144→|--------|---|------|
   145→| medication_id | string | PK: MED-001 |
   146→| medication_name | string | 薬品名 |
   147→| type | string | 内服/外用/注射 |
   148→| manufacturer | string | 製造元 |
   149→| unit_price | decimal | 単価 |
   150→
   151→### raw_insurance_companies (5-8行)
   152→
   153→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   154→|--------|---|------|
   155→| insurance_id | string | PK: INS-001 |
   156→| company_name | string | 会社名 |
   157→| plan_type | string | 国保/社保/共済 |
   158→
   159→### raw_visits (50-80行)
   160→
   161→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   162→|--------|---|------|
   163→| visit_id | string | PK: VIS-001 |
   164→| patient_id | string | FK → raw_patients |
   165→| doctor_id | string | FK → raw_doctors |
   166→| department_id | string | FK → raw_departments |
   167→| visit_date | date | 診察日 |
   168→| diagnosis_code | string | 診断コード |
   169→| diagnosis_name | string | 診断名 |
   170→| notes | string | 備考 |
   171→| treatment_cost | decimal | 治療費 |
   172→
   173→### raw_prescriptions (40-60行)
   174→
   175→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   176→|--------|---|------|
   177→| prescription_id | string | PK: PRE-001 |
   178→| visit_id | string | FK → raw_visits |
   179→| medication_id | string | FK → raw_medications |
   180→| dosage | string | 用量 |
   181→| frequency | string | 頻度 (1日3回等) |
   182→| duration_days | integer | 処方日数 |
   183→
   184→### raw_patient_insurance (25-35行)
   185→
   186→| カラム | 型 | 説明 |
   187→|--------|---|------|
   188→| patient_id | string | FK → raw_patients |
   189→| insurance_id | string | FK → raw_insurance_companies |
   190→| policy_number | string | 保険証番号 |
   191→| start_date | date | 適用開始日 |
   192→| end_date | date | 適用終了日 (NULL=現在有効) |
   193→
   194→## フェーズ分割
   195→
   196→### Phase 1: Raw Vault Core (TA-370)
   197→
   198→**スコープ**: patients + doctors + visits のみ
   199→**成果物**: Hub 3, Link 1, Satellite 3, automate-dvセットアップ
   200→**学習ポイント**: Hub/Link/Satの基本、automate-dv stage macro、ハッシュキー
   201→
   202→### Phase 2: Raw Vault 拡張 (TA-371)
   203→
   204→**スコープ**: departments + medications + insurance + prescriptions + patient_insurance
   205→**成果物**: Hub 2, Link 2, Satellite 4, Effectivity Satellite 1
   206→**学習ポイント**: 標準Link (2キー), Effectivity Satellite, 既存Vault拡張
   207→
   208→### Phase 3: Business Vault (TA-372)
   209→
   210→**スコープ**: PIT + Bridge + Computed Satellite
   211→**成果物**: PITテーブル 1, Bridgeテーブル 1, Computed Satellite 1
   212→**学習ポイント**: クエリ最適化パターン、ビジネスルール適用
   213→
   214→### Phase 4: Information Mart (TA-373)
   215→
   216→**スコープ**: Dim + Fact テーブル
   217→**成果物**: Dimension 4, Fact 2, Wide 1 (optional)
   218→**学習ポイント**: Data Vault → Star Schema変換、SCD Type 2
   219→
   220→## 決定事項
   221→
   222→- **プロジェクト**: transform/fusion (Databricks)
   223→- **データドメイン**: 病院・医療（新規Seed）
   224→- **ツール**: automate-dv パッケージ
   225→- **既存モデルへの影響**: なし（新規ディレクトリで独立）
   226→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/dim_date.sql",
  "old_string": "WITH date_range AS (\n    SELECT\n        MIN(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS min_date,\n        MAX(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS max_date\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n),\n\ndate_series AS (\n    SELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL 1 DAY)) AS date_day\n    FROM date_range\n)",

}

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
`dim_date` がデータ（`sat_visit_details`）に依存していると、初回ビルドで Seed データが無い場合や、将来のデータ追加時にカバーできない日付が生まれます。dbt variable で固定範囲を指定すれば、`dbt_project.yml` で `vars.dim_date_start` を上書きするだけで柔軟に制御できます。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

    11→{"body":"![high](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/high-priority.svg)\n\n`medication_sat`と`visit_sat`への結合に`LEFT JOIN`が使用されていますが、処方箋は必ず薬品と診察に紐づくはずです。`LEFT JOIN`では、対応する薬品や診察のサテライトレコードが存在しない場合にメジャー（`ESTIMATED_COST`など）が`NULL`になる可能性があり、データの不整合につながる恐れがあります。データの整合性を保証するために、これらの結合を`INNER JOIN`に変更することをお勧めします。\n\n```sql\nINNER JOIN medication_sat ms\n    ON pl.MEDICATION_HK = ms.MEDICATION_HK\n    AND ms.rn = 1\nINNER JOIN visit_sat vs\n    ON pl.VISIT_HK = vs.VISIT_HK\n    AND vs.rn = 1\n```","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:15Z","id":2827951319,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/fct_prescriptions.sql","user":"gemini-code-assist[bot]"}
    12→{"body":"![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)\n\n設計ドキュメントと実装の間にいくつかの不整合が見られます。アーキテクチャの概要を正確に保つために、ドキュメントを更新することをお勧めします。\n- **Hubs**: ドキュメントでは5つ (`patient`, `doctor`, `department`, `medication`, `insurance_company`) と記載されていますが、実装には `hub_visit` を含む6つのHubが存在します。\n- **Satellites**: ドキュメントでは7つ (`*_details` + `eff_sat_patient_insurance`) と記載されていますが、実装には9つのSatellite (`sat_visit_details` と `sat_patient_insurance_details` を含む) が存在します。","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:15Z","id":2827951326,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"docs/plans/2026-02-19-datavault-medical-design.md","user":"gemini-code-assist[bot]"}
    13→{"body":"![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)\n\n`as_of_dates` CTE内の各`SELECT`文で`DISTINCT`を使用していますが、`UNION`演算子はデフォルトで結果セットから重複行を削除するため、これらの`DISTINCT`は冗長です。クエリを簡潔にし、わずかながらパフォーマンスを向上させるために、`DISTINCT`を削除することをお勧めします。\n\n```sql\n    SELECT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE\n    FROM {{ ref('hub_patient') }}\n\n    UNION\n\n    SELECT LOAD_DATETIME AS AS_OF_DATE\n    FROM {{ ref('sat_patient_details') }}\n```","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:15Z","id":2827951329,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/models/business_vault/pit_patient.sql","user":"gemini-code-assist[bot]"}
    14→{"body":"![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)\n\n最終的な`SELECT`文において、`COALESCE`と`CAST`は冗長です。`WHERE spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS IS NOT NULL`句によって`SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS`が`NULL`である行はすべて除外されるため、`COALESCE`が評価されることはありません。クエリを簡潔にするために、`COALESCE`と`CAST`を削除することをお勧めします。\n\n```sql\n    spl.SAT_PATIENT_DETAILS_LDTS\n```","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:15Z","id":2827951336,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/models/business_vault/pit_patient.sql","user":"gemini-code-assist[bot]"}
    15→{"body":"![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)\n\n`LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK`のdescriptionが、`stg_prescriptions.sql`での実際の定義と一致していません。SQLファイルでは`VISIT_ID`、`MEDICATION_ID`、`PRESCRIPTION_ID`の3つのキーからハッシュが生成されていますが、descriptionでは2つのキー（`VISIT_ID + MEDICATION_ID`）から生成されると記載されています。ドキュメントの正確性を保つために、descriptionを修正してください。\n\n```yaml\n        description: Link用複合ハッシュキー（VISIT_ID + MEDICATION_ID + PRESCRIPTION_ID の MD5）\n```","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:15Z","id":2827951337,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/models/staging/datavault/stg_prescriptions.yml","user":"gemini-code-assist[bot]"}
    16→{"body":"![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)\n\n`doctor_id` にタイポの可能性があります。`DOC-09` となっていますが、他のIDの形式（例: `DOC-001`）と合わせるために `DOC-009` に修正することをお勧めします。\n\n```\nVIS-008,PAT-008,DOC-009,2026-01-12,F32.9,うつ病エピソード,不眠と食欲低下 2週間,4500,2026-01-12,HOSPITAL_SYSTEM\n```","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:36:15Z","id":2827951343,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/seeds/datavault/raw_visits.csv","user":"gemini-code-assist[bot]"}

    20→{"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🧩 Analysis chain\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`dbt-databricks use_materialization_v2 full-refresh incremental DROP TABLE bug`\n\n💡 Result:\n\nWhat you’re seeing is a known behavior/bug pattern in the Spark-derived incremental materialization: on `dbt run --full-refresh` for an **incremental** model, the materialization can **explicitly drop the existing relation** (DROP TABLE via `adapter.drop_relation`) before rebuilding, even when the underlying engine could have used **`CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE`** (notably for Delta). This unnecessarily removes table-level grants/ACLs and can have other side effects. [1]\n\nIn **dbt-databricks**, the newer materialization work gated by `flags: { use_materialization_v2: true }` was introduced in the 1.10 line [2], and later release notes indicate changes specifically aimed at avoiding “DROP + CREATE” where `CREATE OR REPLACE` is appropriate and enabling “create or replace semantics on full refresh in Mat V2”. [3] Practically, the remediation path is:\n\n- **Upgrade dbt-databricks to a version that includes the Mat V2 “create/replace on full-refresh” work**, and enable `use_materialization_v2`. [2][3]  \n- If you can’t upgrade, the common workaround is a **custom incremental materialization** that skips `drop_relation` for Delta and relies on create/replace semantics (the approach suggested in the original bug report). [1]\n\n**Sources:**  \n[1] dbt-spark CT-220 issue mirror describing the `drop_relation`-before-rebuild behavior and its consequences (grants removed) — https://lightrun.com/answers/dbt-labs-dbt-spark-ct-220-dont-drop-table-first-for-incremental-full-refresh-if-using-delta-file-format  \n[2] dbt-databricks v1.10.0 release summary noting introduction of `use_materialization_v2` — https://newreleases.io/project/github/databricks/dbt-databricks/release/v1.10.0  \n[3] dbt-databricks v1.11.0 release summary noting “Materialized views now uses CREATE OR REPLACE … instead of DROP + CREATE” and “Allow create or replace semantics on full refresh in Mat V2” — https://newreleases.io/project/github/databricks/dbt-databricks/release/v1.11.0\n\n---\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`dbt-databricks use_materialization_v2 view schema default resolved`\n\n💡 Result:\n\n`use_materialization_v2` in **dbt-databricks** changes *how* views are updated (for example, `view_update_via_alter` / safer swaps), but it **does not change how the target schema is chosen**; schema (and catalog) resolution is still standard dbt naming logic. [3]\n\n### How the **schema** is resolved for a `view` when `schema` is “default”\nIn dbt, “default” effectively means **no custom schema was supplied**, so dbt uses your **target schema** (`target.schema`) from the profile. [1]\n\nIf you *do* set `schema: \u003ccustom\u003e`, dbt’s default behavior is to build in:\n`\u003ctarget_schema\u003e_\u003ccustom_schema\u003e` (via the `generate_schema_name` macro). [1]\n\n### How that maps on **Databricks / Unity Catalog**\nWith `dbt-databricks`, your profile defines:\n- `catalog:` (optional) → the Unity Catalog **catalog** (dbt often calls this the “database” conceptually)\n- `schema:` → the Unity Catalog **schema** where models (including `view`s) are created by default [2]\n\nSo a view ends up at:\n- `\u003ccatalog\u003e.\u003ctarget_schema\u003e.\u003cview_name\u003e` if `schema` is default/none\n- `\u003ccatalog\u003e.\u003ctarget_schema\u003e_\u003ccustom_schema\u003e.\u003cview_name\u003e` if `schema: \u003ccustom_schema\u003e` is set (unless you override `generate_schema_name`). [1][2]\n\n---\n\n🌐 Web query:\n\n`dbt-databricks use_materialization_v2 experimental status 2025 2026`\n\n💡 Result:\n\n`use_materialization_v2` in **dbt-databricks** is an **opt-in behavior/feature flag** that gates the “Materialization V2” revamps (tables/views/seeds, safer replace patterns, etc.). It was introduced in the **1.10.0** line (alpha pre-release first, then 1.10.0 GA) and, as of the latest 2026-era releases, it is **still controlled by the flag (i.e., not the default behavior)**—so functionally it remains **“experimental/rollout-gated” rather than fully default-on**. [1][2][3]\n\n**Timeline (key points)**\n- **Nov 19, 2024**: PR merged introducing the V2 materialization gating flag. [4]\n- **dbt-databricks 1.10.0 (Apr 8, 2025)**: changelog explicitly notes “Introduced `use_materialization_v2` flag for gating materialization revamps.” [2]\n- **Jan 12, 2026**: PyPI shows latest release **1.11.4**, indicating the adapter is well past initial rollout but still uses flags for behavior gating (including this one). [3]\n\n## Sources\n[1] dbt-databricks v1.10.0a1 release notes (NewReleases) — “Introduced use_materialization_v2 flag…” https://newreleases.io/project/github/databricks/dbt-databricks/release/v1.10.0a1  \n[2] dbt-databricks CHANGELOG.md (raw) — 1.10.0 entry introducing the flag https://raw.githubusercontent.com/databricks/dbt-databricks/main/CHANGELOG.md  \n[3] dbt-databricks on PyPI — release history showing 1.11.4 on Jan 12, 2026 https://pypi.org/project/dbt-databricks/  \n[4] GitHub PR `#844` — “V2 Materialization flag introduced” (merged Nov 19, 2024) https://github.com/databricks/dbt-databricks/pull/844\n\n---\n\n\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n**Review use_materialization_v2 flag status; original concerns have been addressed in recent dbt-databricks releases.**\n\nThe `--full-refresh` issue for incremental models has been resolved since dbt-databricks 1.11.0 (released with \"create or replace semantics on full refresh in Mat V2\"), which specifically fixes the `DROP TABLE` problem by using `CREATE OR REPLACE` instead. With current versions (1.11.4+), enabling `use_materialization_v2` is actually the recommended approach to avoid the original full-refresh issue.\n\nThe schema claim requires clarification: `use_materialization_v2` does not change how target schemas are resolved. View schema assignment still follows standard dbt naming logic via the `generate_schema_name` macro, regardless of whether Mat V2 is enabled.\n\nThe flag remains opt-in by design (not enabled by default) as a deliberate rollout gate, not due to active unresolved bugs. If targeting a recent dbt-databricks version, consider enabling this flag to benefit from the full-refresh fix and other materialization improvements.\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🤖 Prompt for AI Agents\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@transform/fusion/dbt_project.yml` around lines 6 - 8, The review says the\nuse_materialization_v2 flag should be enabled for modern dbt-databricks versions\nbecause the full-refresh DROP TABLE issue was fixed in 1.11.0+, so update the\nflags block by setting use_materialization_v2: true (or ensure it remains true)\nin the dbt_project.yml flags section and remove or update any accompanying\ncomment that incorrectly claims Mat V2 changes schema resolution; explicitly\nstate that schema resolution still uses generate_schema_name and that Mat V2 is\nopt-in by design for rollout gating.\n```\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro --\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit --\u003e","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:46:45Z","id":2827999511,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":8,"path":"transform/fusion/dbt_project.yml","user":"coderabbitai[bot]"}
    21→{"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n**`FIRST_VISIT_DATE`/`LAST_VISIT_DATE` reflect ETL load time, not clinical visit dates.**\n\n`LOAD_DATETIME` from `sat_visit_details` is a technical vault metadata field recording when the row was ingested — it is not a business-meaningful visit date. Using `MIN`/`MAX` of it will produce the earliest/latest *load timestamps*, which could simply reflect batch scheduling windows rather than when patients actually attended appointments. Downstream consumers of `dim_patient` (which references this satellite) will receive misleading values for these two columns.\n\nThe actual visit date should come from a business attribute column in the satellite (e.g., `VISIT_DATE`) or from the source data captured at staging time. If no explicit business date exists yet in `sat_visit_details`, that attribute needs to be added to the satellite's source schema and propagated through staging.\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🐛 Illustrative fix (assuming a `VISIT_DATE` column exists in `sat_visit_details`)\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```diff\n visit_details_latest AS (\n     SELECT\n         VISIT_HK,\n         DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n         TREATMENT_COST,\n+        VISIT_DATE,\n         LOAD_DATETIME,\n         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (\n             PARTITION BY VISIT_HK\n             ORDER BY LOAD_DATETIME DESC\n         ) AS rn\n     FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n ),\n\n patient_visits AS (\n     SELECT\n         lv.PATIENT_HK,\n         lv.VISIT_HK,\n         lv.DOCTOR_HK,\n         svd.DIAGNOSIS_CODE,\n         svd.TREATMENT_COST,\n+        svd.VISIT_DATE,\n         svd.LOAD_DATETIME\n     FROM {{ ref('link_visit') }} lv\n     INNER JOIN visit_details_latest svd\n         ON lv.VISIT_HK = svd.VISIT_HK\n         AND svd.rn = 1\n ),\n ...\n visit_summary AS (\n     SELECT\n         ...\n-        MIN(LOAD_DATETIME) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE,\n-        MAX(LOAD_DATETIME) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE\n+        MIN(VISIT_DATE) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE,\n+        MAX(VISIT_DATE) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE\n     FROM patient_visits\n     GROUP BY PATIENT_HK\n ),\n```\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🤖 Prompt for AI Agents\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@transform/fusion/models/business_vault/sat_patient_visit_summary.sql` around\nlines 45 - 46, The MIN/MAX aggregation is using the technical LOAD_DATETIME from\nsat_visit_details to populate FIRST_VISIT_DATE and LAST_VISIT_DATE, which gives\nETL timestamps instead of business visit dates; change the aggregations to use\nthe business date attribute (e.g., VISIT_DATE) from sat_visit_details (e.g.,\nMIN(VISIT_DATE) AS FIRST_VISIT_DATE, MAX(VISIT_DATE) AS LAST_VISIT_DATE) and\nensure dim_patient references these corrected fields; if VISIT_DATE is missing\nin sat_visit_details, add that attribute to the satellite source schema and\npropagate it through staging before computing FIRST_VISIT_DATE/LAST_VISIT_DATE.\n```\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro --\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit --\u003e","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:46:46Z","id":2827999521,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/models/business_vault/sat_patient_visit_summary.sql","user":"coderabbitai[bot]"}
    22→{"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n**`dim_date` date range is too narrow and silently produces an empty table when `sat_visit_details` is empty.**\n\nTwo related problems:\n\n1. **Narrow coverage**: The date range is bounded by `sat_visit_details.EFFECTIVE_FROM`. `fct_prescriptions` references `PRESCRIPTION_DATE` as a foreign key into `dim_date`, but prescription dates that fall outside the min/max visit date range will have no matching `dim_date` row, silently breaking star-schema joins.\n\n2. **Silent empty table**: In Spark SQL, `SEQUENCE(NULL, NULL, INTERVAL 1 DAY)` returns `NULL`, and `EXPLODE(NULL)` emits zero rows. If `sat_visit_details` is empty (first load, dev environment, CI), `dim_date` is entirely empty, causing all downstream dimension lookups to return nothing.\n\nA date dimension should be independent of any single fact source and should cover a wide, predefined range. Use dbt variables to parameterize the bounds:\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e💡 Proposed fix\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```diff\n-WITH date_range AS (\n-    SELECT\n-        MIN(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS min_date,\n-        MAX(CAST(EFFECTIVE_FROM AS DATE)) AS max_date\n-    FROM {{ ref('sat_visit_details') }}\n-),\n-\n-date_series AS (\n-    SELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(min_date, max_date, INTERVAL 1 DAY)) AS date_day\n-    FROM date_range\n-)\n+WITH date_series AS (\n+    SELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(\n+        CAST('{{ var(\"date_dim_start\", \"2020-01-01\") }}' AS DATE),\n+        CAST('{{ var(\"date_dim_end\",  \"2030-12-31\") }}' AS DATE),\n+        INTERVAL 1 DAY\n+    )) AS date_day\n+)\n```\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🤖 Prompt for AI Agents\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n```\nVerify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.\n\nIn `@transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/dim_date.sql` around lines 1 - 11,\nThe current dim_date CTEs (date_range, date_series) bound the calendar to\nsat_visit_details.EFFECTIVE_FROM and call SEQUENCE(min_date, max_date), which\nyields NULL/empty when that table is empty and misses PRESCRIPTION_DATE values\noutside that narrow window; update dim_date to use dbt variables for absolute\nbounds (e.g., var('dim_date_min'), var('dim_date_max') with sensible defaults)\nand COALESCE the computed min/max from the date_range into those vars so\nSEQUENCE always receives non-NULL start/end; ensure the CTEs (date_range,\ndate_series) reference these coalesced dates (cast to DATE) so the date series\nspans the full desired range independent of sat_visit_details and covers\nPRESCRIPTION_DATE from fct_prescriptions.\n```\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- fingerprinting:phantom:poseidon:churro --\u003e\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment by CodeRabbit --\u003e\n\n✅ Addressed in commits b0b6da9 to a30c668","created_at":"2026-02-19T13:46:46Z","id":2827999532,"in_reply_to_id":null,"line":null,"path":"transform/fusion/models/marts/datavault/dim_date.sql","user":"coderabbitai[bot]"}
    23→{"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e🧩 Analysis chain\u003c/summary\u003e\n\n🏁 Script executed:\n\n```shell\nfind . -type f -name '*eff_sat_patient_insurance*' -o -type f -path '*patient_insurance*' -name '*.sql' | head -20\n```\n\nRepository: ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare\n\nLength of output: 457\n\n---\n\n🏁 Script executed:\n\n```shell\ncat -n ./transform/fusion/models/raw_vault/satellites/eff_sat_patient_insurance.sql\n```\n\nRepository: ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare\n\nLength of output: 964\n\n---\n\n🏁 Script executed:\n\n```shell\ncat -n ./transform/fusion/models/staging/datavault/stg_patient_insurance.sql\n```\n\nRepository: ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare\n\nLength of output: 1285\n\n---\n\n🏁 Script executed:\n\n```shell\ncat -n ./transform/fusion/models/raw_vault/satellites/eff_sat_patient_insurance.yml\n```\n\nRepository: ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare\n\nLength of output: 1017\n\n---\n\n\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n**Add `EFFECTIVE_FROM` to the YAML schema columns.**\n\nThe SQL file passes `src_eff=\"EFFECTIVE_FROM\"` to the `automate_dv.eff_sat` macro (line 9), and the upstream staging model `stg_patient_insurance` provides this column (line 6).     24→{"body":"_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_\n\n**PII/PHI payload — ensure data-at-rest protections are in place before any production use.**\n\nThe `src_payload` bundles direct patient identifiers (`FIRST_NAME`, `LAST_NAME`, `DATE_OF_BIRTH`, `ADDRESS`, `PHONE`) that constitute PHI under HIPAA and personal data under GDPR. **新しい対応が必要なコメント:**

| # | ファイル | 指摘 | 重要度 |
|---|---------|------|--------|
| 1 | `pit_patient.sql` | business_vault のmaterialization未設定（viewになる） | Major |
| 2 | 設計ドキュメント | fenced code blockに言語指定なし | Minor |
| 3 | 設計ドキュメント | ディレクトリ一覧に hub_visit, sat_patient_insurance_details が欠落 | Minor |
| 4 | 設計ドキュメント | Phase 1/2 の Hub数不整合 | Minor |
| 5 | `dim_patient_completeness.sql` | SELECTにPATIENT_ID（PII）が含まれている | Minor |
| 6 | `dbt_project.yml` use_materialization_v2 | 情報提供のみ（既に設定済み） | - |
| 7 | PHI/PII、HASHDIFF | 学習プロジェクトのため対応不要 | Skip |

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
dbtの singular test は「行が返ればテスト失敗」という仕組みなので、SELECT するカラムはデバッグ用途のみ。ハッシュキーだけで十分であり、PII（患者IDなど）をSELECTすると、CIログやDatabricksクエリ履歴に漏れるリスクがあります。
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

WITH date_series AS (
    SELECT EXPLODE(SEQUENCE(
        CAST('2024-01-01' AS DATE),
        CAST('2026-12-31' AS DATE),
        INTERVAL 1 DAY
    )) AS date_day
)

SELECT
    CAST(date_day AS DATE) AS DATE_KEY,
    YEAR(date_day) AS YEAR,
    MONTH(date_day) AS MONTH,
    DAY(date_day) AS DAY_OF_MONTH,
    DAYOFWEEK(date_day) AS DAY_OF_WEEK,
    CASE DAYOFWEEK(date_day)
        WHEN 1 THEN '日'
        WHEN 2 THEN '月'
        WHEN 3 THEN '火'
        WHEN 4 THEN '水'
        WHEN 5 THEN '木'
        WHEN 6 THEN '金'
        WHEN 7 THEN '土'
    END AS DAY_NAME_JP,
    WEEKOFYEAR(date_day) AS WEEK_OF_YEAR,
    QUARTER(date_day) AS QUARTER,
    CASE
        WHEN DAYOFWEEK(date_day) IN (1, 7) THEN FALSE
        ELSE TRUE
    END AS IS_WEEKDAY,
    DATE_FORMAT(date_day, 'yyyy-MM') AS YEAR_MONTH
FROM date_series
```

## PR #46 レビュー対応完了サマリー

### 対応済みの修正（2コミット）

**コミット 1** (`a30c668`): Gemini / CodeRabbit 第1ラウンド
1. `fct_prescriptions.sql`: LEFT JOIN → INNER JOIN
2. `pit_patient.sql`: 冗長な DISTINCT 削除
3. `sat_patient_visit_summary.sql`: LOAD_DATETIME → EFFECTIVE_FROM
4. `dim_date.sql`: データ依存範囲 → dbt variable ベース固定範囲
5. `stg_prescriptions.yml`: LINK_PRESCRIPTION_HK 説明修正
6. `eff_sat_patient_insurance.yml`: EFFECTIVE_FROM カラム追加
7. 設計ドキュメント: Hub数 5→6、Satellite数 7→9

**コミット 2** (`8099bea`): CodeRabbit 第2ラウンド
1. 設計ドキュメント: fenced code block に `text` 言語指定追加
2. 設計ドキュメント: ディレクトリ一覧に `hub_visit.sql` / `sat_patient_insurance_details.sql` 追加
3. 設計ドキュメント: Phase 2 成果物数 Hub 2→3、Satellite 4→5
4. `dim_patient_completeness.sql`: PII（PATIENT_ID）を SELECT から除外

### スキップした指摘
- **PHI/PII暗号化**: 学習プロジェクトのため対応不要
- **HASHDIFF churn（Satellite分割）**: 学習プロジェクトのため対応不要
- **pit_patient materialization**: `dbt_project.yml` で `business_vault: +materialized: table` 設定済み（CodeRabbit の誤検知）
- **use_materialization_v2**: 既に `true` で設定済み（情報提供のみ）

### CI状態
- GitGuardian: **pass**
- claude-review: **pending** (実行中)
- dbt Cloud: **fail** (CI環境でのテーブル不存在の可能性。ローカルではコンパイル成功)

claude-review の完了と dbt Cloud failure の原因確認を待ってから、マージを判断してください。
